*Nicolas Floquet,Joseph Le Roux,Nadi Tomeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散模型训练以完整标签序列为条件的CRF，结合近似CRF推理，在词性标注任务上实现16.5%的错误率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性链CRF假设有限决策跨度（如标签二元组），限制了表达能力和处理长距离依赖的性能。作者试图通过扩散方法突破这一限制。

Method: 利用扩散模型训练一个以整个标签序列为条件的CRF，但条件作用于带噪声的标签版本；结合近似CRF推理进行解码。

Result: 在词性标注任务上，该方法实现了16.5%的错误率降低，显著提升了标签准确率。

Conclusion: 扩散机制可以有效增强CRF对长距离依赖的建模能力，在序列标注任务中优于传统神经CRF方法。

Abstract: Sequence labelling, a core task of Natural Language Processing (NLP), consists in assigning each token of an input sentence a label.
  From a Machine Learning point of view, sequence labelling is often cast as a Linear-Chain Conditional Random Field (CRF) parametrised by a neural network.
  While this approach gives good empirical results, CRFs assume a finite decision span (eg label bigrams) which can limit their expressivity and hurt performance when long-range dependencies are required.
  We show we can leverage diffusion to train a CRF conditioned on an entire label sequence, with the caveat that the condition is on a noisy version of labels.
  We show experimentally that this method, in conjunction with approximate CRF inference, improves label accuracy with a 16.5% error reduction for POS-tagging.

</details>


### [36] [Efficient Financial Language Understanding via Distillation with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18875)
*Wen-Fong,Huang,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过合成数据蒸馏的高效金融情感分析框架，利用聚类种子选择方法，使小型学生模型在极少人工标注下达到甚至超越大型教师模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型指令微调模型在金融领域部署成本高，且受限于数据保密性和专家标注成本，标注数据稀缺。

Method: 从大型指令微调教师模型中蒸馏知识至紧凑学生模型；先人工标注少量真实样本，通过聚类选择种子示例，再使用结构化少样本提示生成合成数据。

Result: 基于聚类的种子选择比随机采样生成更具代表性的合成数据；在复杂嘈杂文本领域，基于完整合成语料训练的学生模型甚至超越教师模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为金融NLP中低资源领域适配提供了实用路径，显著减少了人工标注工作量。

Abstract: Large instruction-following models are powerful but costly to deploy, particularly in finance, where labelled data are limited by confidentiality and expert annotation cost. We present an efficient framework for financial sentiment analysis through distillation with synthetic data, transferring knowledge from a large instruction-tuned teacher to compact student models. The framework is designed for low-resource conditions, where a small set of real examples are collected and labelled by hand. The framework then clusters the examples and uses the clusters to select seeds for generating synthetic examples via structured few-shot prompting. Experiments show that clustering-based seed selection yields more representative synthetic data than random sampling, enabling compact models to achieve strong performance with minimal supervision. Notably, on a more complex and noisy text domain, the compact model trained on the complete synthetic-seed corpus even outperforms the teacher model, while remaining competitive on formal text. The framework provides a practical route toward resource-efficient domain adaptation in financial NLP with minimal human labelling effort.

</details>


### [37] [Improving Medical Communication using Rubric-Guided Counterfactual Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18889)
*Adrian Cosma,Nicoleta-Nina Basoc,Andrei Niculae,Cosmin Dumitrache,Emilian Radoi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型引导的反事实推荐流程，通过发现和优化可解释的沟通特征（如语气、个性化、可操作性、完整性），在不干扰医疗内容的前提下，最小化地调整医患沟通方式，从而提升患者积极反馈概率。实验表明，该方法在独立审计模型下平均提升+6.41%的积极反馈概率，93.31%的推荐为非负收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的远程医疗中的患者反馈主要反映感知沟通质量而非医学准确性，缺乏对可解释沟通特征的系统性优化方法，因此需要一种能在不影响医学内容的前提下，通过微小沟通调整提升患者满意度的方案。

Method: 构建LM引导的反事实推荐流程：首先使用语言模型发现并提炼可解释的沟通特征（语气、个性化、可操作性、完整性）；将这些特征与医患互动元数据结合，训练正反馈预测模型；在推理时，系统搜索低成本的序数特征变化空间，推荐能够提升正反馈概率的最小沟通调整方案；最后使用独立审计模型验证推荐效果的泛化性。

Result: 在独立审计模型下，推荐的沟通调整平均带来+6.41%的正反馈概率提升；93.31%的推荐为非负收益（即未降低反馈概率）。

Conclusion: 微小、可解释的沟通变化能够捕获大部分预测收益，同时保留医生对医学推理和最终措辞的控制权，表明该方法在提升患者体验方面具有实用价值。

Abstract: Text-based telemedicine increasingly relies on lightweight patient feedback, however, such feedback primarily reflects perceived communication quality rather than medical accuracy. We introduce an LM-guided counterfactual recommendation pipeline that discovers and refines interpretable communication features such as tone, personalization, actionability and completeness in addressing patient concerns, without interfering with the medical content. These features are used together with patient-doctor interaction metadata to estimate positive feedback. At inference time, the system searches over low-cost ordinal feature changes and recommends minimal communication changes predicted to increase the probability of positive feedback, while independent auditor models test whether these gains generalize beyond the selection model. Across interactions, recommendations yield a mean +6.41% gain in predicted positive feedback probability under independent auditors, and are non-negative for 93.31% of recommendations. These results suggest that small, interpretable communication changes can capture most predicted gains while preserving the doctor's control over medical reasoning and final wording.

</details>


### [38] [Learning Robust Pair Confidence for Multimodal Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18893)
*Zhuangzhuang Pan,Ning Dong,Yingna Su,Yan Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RPCL（鲁棒对置信度学习）框架，通过置信度差分边界约束和局部表示扰动对齐，改善多模态情感-原因对提取中的对置信度脆弱性问题，在三个基准数据集上提升Pair F1 2.58-2.83个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对评分器使用对级交叉熵损失，将候选对视为独立个体，导致相对置信度几何结构约束不足，Gold对与难负例距离过近或依赖偶然的非Gold上下文，存在对置信度脆弱性问题。

Method: RPCL包含两项训练目标：(1) 置信度差分边界约束——将Gold对与行方向难负例分离；(2) 置信度对齐——在部分干扰非Gold上下文表示后，对齐干净预测与损坏视图的预测。推理时使用原始评分器和解码流程不变。

Result: 在ECF、MECAD、MEC4三个数据集上，全文本-音频-视频设置下，三种子随机种子的平均Pair F1提升2.58-2.83个百分点，Pair AUPRC在所有数据集上均有提升。诊断分析显示Gold-负例置信度差距更大、边界违反程度更低。

Conclusion: 显式塑造对置信度是MECPE任务的有效训练策略，RPCL能在不改变推理结构的前提下提升对置信度的判别性和稳定性。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion-cause pair extraction (MECPE) requires reliable pair confidence over candidate pairs. Existing pair scorers commonly use pair-level cross entropy over valid candidates, which treats links mostly independently. This leaves the relative confidence geometry among competing causes under-constrained, allowing gold pairs to stay close to hard negatives or rely on incidental non-gold context. We study this vulnerability as pair-confidence brittleness and propose RPCL (Robust Pair Confidence Learning), a training-only framework for pair-confidence learning. RPCL encourages pair confidence to be both discriminative and stable: gold pairs are separated from row-wise hard negatives through a confidence-difference margin constraint, and clean pair predictions are aligned with predictions from a corrupted view where non-gold contextual utterance representations are partially corrupted. The original clean pair scorer and decoding pipeline are used unchanged at inference time. On ECF, MECAD, and MEC4, RPCL improves the three-seed mean Pair F1 over a matched base model by 2.58 to 2.83 percentage points in the full text-audio-video setting, and improves mean Pair AUPRC on all three datasets. Diagnostic analysis further shows larger gold-negative confidence gaps and lower margin-violation severity. These results suggest that explicitly shaping pair confidence is an effective training strategy for MECPE.

</details>


### [39] [SAGE: Stochastic Prompt Optimization via Agent-Guided Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18902)
*Ziyi Zhu,Luka Smyth,Saki Shinoda,Jinghong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPO是一个随机提示词优化框架，通过三种策略（错误引导随机搜索、遗传算法、多智能体SAGE）在无参数更新的情况下优化AI系统。实验发现没有单一策略占优，效果取决于错误类型与优化空间结构的交互。在心理健康聊天机器人上，SAGE通过连续优化将8轮噪声A/B测试转化为显著的次日留存提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本梯度并非真实梯度，因此自动提示词优化应被视为黑盒搜索问题。现有方法缺乏对此类问题的系统性探索，需要更有效的随机搜索框架。

Method: 提出SPO框架，包含三种策略：1) 错误引导随机搜索；2) 带进化算子的遗传算法；3) SAGE——多智能体管道，结合诊断性代码执行进行智能探索与优化。

Result: 三个基准测试上无单一策略占优；SAGE在心理健康聊天机器人上通过8轮连续A/B测试实现次日留存的统计学显著提升。

Conclusion: 定性诊断与定量验证相结合是智能体优化在开放式任务导向对话中见效的关键。提示词优化没有通用最优策略，需根据错误类型和优化空间结构选择方法。

Abstract: Context engineering has emerged as a primary lever for improving AI systems without parameter updates. Recent work showing that textual gradients do not function as real gradients motivates treating automatic prompt optimization (APO) as black-box search. We introduce SPO (Stochastic Prompt Optimization), a framework for stochastic search over prompt space, and compare three strategies of increasing sophistication: error-informed random search, a genetic algorithm with evolutionary operators, and SAGE (SPO via Agent-Guided Exploration), a multi-agent pipeline with diagnostic code execution. Across three benchmarks, no single strategy dominates; effectiveness depends on the interaction of landscape structure with error type. We further deploy SAGE on a mental-health chatbot under a continuous optimization paradigm, where it compounds eight cycles of individually-noisy A/B tests into a statistically robust gain in next-day retention. We argue that coupling qualitative diagnosis with quantitative validation is what makes agentic optimization effective for open-ended task-oriented dialogue.

</details>


### [40] [As Easy as Rocket Science: Assessing the Ability of Large Language Models to Interpret Negation in Figurative Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18922)
*Jasmine Owers,Edwin Simpson,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理同时包含否定和比喻语言文本时的能力。作者通过对现有比喻语言数据集进行新的注释，并测试多种语言模型，发现否定与比喻的结合对模型构成特殊挑战，且模型性能高度依赖于提示风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比喻语言和否定是当前语言模型面临的两大挑战，但两者在日常语言中广泛使用。由于LLMs在实际应用中无法针对特定数据集进行微调，因此理解它们正确解读同时包含否定和比喻语言文本的能力至关重要。

Method: 对现有比喻语言数据集开发新的注释集（标注否定信息），并在该数据集上测试多种语言模型。

Result: 否定与比喻语言的结合对模型构成特殊挑战；模型在不同否定类型上的整体表现高度依赖于所使用的提示风格。

Conclusion: LLMs在处理否定与比喻语言并存的情况时面临显著困难，提示设计是影响性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Figurative language and negation are two areas that challenge current language models, however, both are widely used throughout written and spoken language. Large language models (LLMs) are also widely used in everyday contexts where they cannot necessarily be tuned for a specific dataset. It is therefore essential to understand the ability of LLMs to correctly interpret text that includes both negation and figurative language. To investigate this, we develop a set of new annotations to an existing dataset of figurative language, and test a range of language models on the dataset. We find that the combination of negation and figurativeness can present a particular challenge, and that performance overall and across different negation types is particularly dependent on the prompt style used.

</details>


### [41] [SenFlow: Inter-Sentence Flow Modeling for AI-Generated Text Detection in Hybrid Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18946)
*Jingkun Luo,Yifan Sun,Da-Tian Peng,Guanxiong Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC基准（含16,000篇混合文档）和SenFlow方法，通过图传播与线性链CRF的文档级结构化预测，解决句子级AI文本检测中忽视句子间依赖性和缺少最新生成器的问题，在跨域迁移上平均F1提升4.15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级AI文本检测（S-AGTD）存在两个缺口：1）逐句孤立分类，忽视句子间依赖关系；2）现有基准未包含最新一代生成器（如DeepSeek-V3.2和Kimi K2）。

Method: 构建MOSAIC基准（覆盖PubMed和XSum，经困惑度一致性过滤）；将S-AGTD重定义为文档句子序列上的结构化预测，提出SenFlow——在句子图上进行基于图的句子间传播与线性链CRF解码的单文档级前向传递。

Result: SenFlow在MOSAIC上达到SOTA，跨域迁移（最难协议）平均Macro-F1提升+4.15个百分点；即使经过困惑度过滤均衡显性线索后，AI插入仍存在生成器相关的句子长度差异，句子级检测器仍可利用。

Conclusion: 文档级结构化预测能有效捕捉句子间依赖，提升混合文档中AI文本检测性能；句子长度差异可作为鲁棒线索，但需关注生成器依赖的偏差。

Abstract: Sentence-level AI-generated text detection (S-AGTD) for hybrid documents, where humans and LLMs co-author one text, faces two gaps: existing methods classify each sentence in isolation, discarding inter-sentence dependencies, and existing benchmarks omit the newest generation of generators. We construct MOSAIC, a benchmark of 16,000 hybrid documents over PubMed and XSum, generated by DeepSeek-V3.2 and Kimi K2 under stringent quality controls including a perplexity-consistency filter absent from prior benchmarks. We recast S-AGTD as structured prediction over the document sentence sequence and instantiate it as SenFlow, integrating graph-based inter-sentence propagation with linear-chain CRF decoding in a single document-level pass over a sentence graph. SenFlow reaches state-of-the-art performance on MOSAIC, with a +4.15 pp average Macro-F1 margin on cross-domain transfer, the hardest of three protocols of increasing difficulty. We further find that even after the perplexity filter equalizes overt cues, AI insertions retain a generator-dependent sentence-length gap that sentence-level detectors still exploit. Code and data: https://github.com/luojingkun22/SenFlow

</details>


### [42] [GraphPO: Graph-based Policy Optimization for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18954)
*Yuliang Zhan,Xinyu Tang,Jian Li,Dandan Zheng,Weilong Chai,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Ge Wu,Wenyue Tang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中独立采样导致冗余探索和奖励稀疏的问题，本文提出GraphPO框架，将推理轨迹建模为有向无环图，通过语义等价路径合并和双向优势函数分配，在相同预算下显著优于链式和树式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法存在两个主要局限：(1) 独立采样时不同轨迹包含相似的中间推理步骤，导致重复探索和计算浪费；(2) 稀疏的最终答案奖励难以识别有效中间步骤。树方法虽通过前缀共享和分支比较提供细粒度信号，但不同分支到达相似推理状态时仍无法共享信息，且仅进行局部比较导致优势估计方差较高。

Method: 提出GraphPO框架，将rollout表示为有向无环图(DAG)，其中推理步作为边，语义状态作为节点。通过将语义等价的推理路径合并为等价类，允许共享后缀并将预算从冗余探索重新分配到多样化探索。同时，对入边分配效率优势，对出边分配正确性优势，从结果中推导出过程监督信号。

Result: 理论分析表明GraphPO降低了优势估计方差并提升了推理效率。在三个LLM上的推理和智能体搜索基准实验中，GraphPO在相同token预算或响应预算下一致优于链式和树式基线方法。

Conclusion: GraphPO通过图结构建模有效解决了RLVR中的冗余探索和奖励稀疏问题，为大型推理模型的强化学习优化提供了更高效、更稳定的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for enhancing the capability of large reasoning models. RLVR typically samples responses independently and optimizes the policy using from final answers. This paradigm has two limitations. First, independently responses often contain similar intermediate reasoning steps, causing redundant exploration and wasted computation. Second, sparse final-answer rewards make it hard to identify useful steps. Tree-based methods partly address this problem by sharing prefixes and comparing branches from the same prefix to provide fine-grained signals. However, tree branches are still expanded independently. When different branches reach similar reasoning states, they cannot share information and repeat similar exploration. Moreover, tree-based methods ignore such dispersion and only perform local comparisons within separate branches, which can lead to higher variance in advantage estimation. To address this challenge, we propose GraphPO (Graph-based Policy Optimization), a novel RL framework that represents rollouts as a directed acyclic graph, with reasoning steps as edges and semantic states summarized from the reasoning paths as nodes. GraphPO merges semantically equivalent reasoning paths into equivalence classes, allowing them to share suffixes and reallocating budget away from redundant expansions to diverse exploration. Furthermore, we assign efficiency advantages to incoming edges and correctness advantages to outgoing edges, thereby improving inference efficiency while deriving process supervision from outcome. Theory shows that GraphPO reduces advantage-estimation variance and enhances reasoning efficiency. Experiments on three LLMs across reasoning and agentic search benchmarks show that GraphPO consistently outperforms chain- and tree-based baselines with the same token budgets or response budgets.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Tokenization: Direct Timestep Embedding and Contrastive Alignment for Time-Series Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18986)
*Yafeng Wu,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CADE框架，通过直接时间步嵌入（点式线性编码器+MLP投影器）和单向监督对比损失对齐时序与文本语义，解决了LLM处理时序序列时的分词瓶颈和粒度固定问题，在Time-MQA基准上超越开源和闭源基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM直接处理原始数值序列存在分词瓶颈（BPE破坏连续数值的度量结构，丢失幅度、尺度和趋势信息），而基于补丁的编码器固定窗口粒度会破坏模式并隐藏精确时间步，且跨数据集迁移性差。

Method: CADE包含两个核心组件：(1) 直接时间步嵌入——通过点式线性编码器和MLP投影器将每个时间步直接映射到LLM嵌入空间，保留索引级访问，消除分块和填充；(2) 语义对齐——引入单向监督对比损失，将时序嵌入与冻结的类别名文本锚点对齐。

Result: 在Time-MQA公开基准上，CADE在六个TSQA任务上持续改进性能，超越了开源和专有LLM基线。

Conclusion: CADE通过直接嵌入和语义对齐有效解决了时序序列的分词瓶颈和粒度问题，为时间序列问答任务提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have given rise to time-series question answering (TSQA), which formulates time-series analysis as natural-language question answering. However, directly feeding raw numerical series into LLMs suffers from a tokenization bottleneck: Byte Pair Encoding fragments continuous values into unstable tokens whose embeddings lack meaningful metric structure, resulting in the loss of magnitude, scale, and trend information. Prior methods use patch-based encoders that split the series into fixed windows, locking in one granularity that breaks patterns and hides exact timesteps, through a separate module that rarely transfers across datasets with different lengths or sampling rates. To address this challenge, we propose CADE (Contrastive Alignment with Direct Embedding), a novel framework for TSQA built upon two key components: direct timestep embedding and semantic alignment. The proposed framework maps each timestep directly into the LLM embedding space through a point-wise linear encoder and MLP projector, preserving exact index-level access while eliminating the need for patching and padding. To further bridge the semantic gap between time-series and language representations, we introduce a novel one-directional supervised contrastive loss that aligns time-series embeddings with frozen class-name text anchors. Experimental results on the public Time-MQA benchmark demonstrate that our framework consistently improves performance across six TSQA tasks, outperforming both open-source and proprietary LLM baselines.

</details>


### [44] [G-IdiomAlign: A Gloss-Pivoted Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Idiom Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18989)
*Fengying Ye,Yanming Sun,Runzhe Zhan,Zheqi Zhang,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: G-IdiomAlign是一个基于释义锚定的习语翻译基准，通过多选和对比生成两种协议评估LLM的习语翻译能力。研究发现LLM普遍存在字面翻译偏差，释义能改善但效果有限，且改进主要归因于注意力头而非层的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语因其非组合性和弱表层形式基础而难以跨语言迁移，字面映射不可靠，现有评估缺乏可控的基准。

Method: 构建G-IdiomAlign基准，以Wiktionary英文释义锚定每个习语；设计两种协议：(1)带类型干扰项的多选习语等价任务；(2)无释义/有释义对比生成任务，通过嵌入语义代理隔离显式语义枢纽的效果。

Result: LLM普遍存在字面翻译偏差（尤其在低资源语言目标下）；释义在对比生成中持续改善性能但效果有限，开放输出空间仍有较大提升空间；Qwen3-8B分析表明，跨条件差异更多集中在注意力头而非层，更好的有释义生成与更强的释义锚定相关。

Conclusion: 当前LLM习语翻译能力不足，字面翻译偏差是主要失败模式；释义锚定有一定帮助但远未解决该问题，注意力头层面的机制分析为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Idioms are difficult to transfer across languages due to their non-compositionality and weak surface-form grounding, making literal mappings unreliable. We present G-IdiomAlign, a gloss-pivoted benchmark where each idiom is anchored by an English gloss from Wiktionary. We further construct a high-confidence reference alignment set for reproducible evaluation. G-IdiomAlign supports two protocols: (1) a controlled Multiple-Choice Idiom Equivalence with typed distractors for error attribution; and (2) a Gloss-Contrastive Generation contrasting No-gloss and With-gloss inputs to isolate the effect of an explicit semantic pivot. Across diverse LLMs, a bias to literal translation is a dominant failure mode, especially when the target is a low-resource language. Glosses consistently improve Gloss-Contrastive Generation under an embedding-based semantic proxy, but performance remains modest, indicating substantial headroom in the open output space. Subsequent analysis on Qwen3-8B further suggests that cross-condition differences are concentrated more in attention heads than in layers, while better With-gloss generations coincide with stronger gloss anchoring.

</details>


### [45] [Enhancing Multilingual Reasoning via Steerable Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19002)
*Zhuoran Li,Rui Xu,Jian Yang,Junnan Liu,Zhijun Chen,Qianren Mao,Hongcheng Guo,Jiaheng Liu,Likang Xiao,Ming Li,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ST-Merge框架，通过门控交叉注意力机制自适应调节源模型贡献，解决模型合并中的冲突问题，在多语言推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法采用"一刀切"策略，无法针对不同输入特征动态调整各源模型的贡献权重，导致源模型之间的冲突无法有效解决，合并后的模型性能次优。

Method: 提出ST-Merge框架，引入门控交叉注意力机制，自适应地加权或过滤两个源模型的特征，实现对各源模型贡献的动态调节。

Result: 在4个多语言推理基准、涵盖21种语言的实验中，ST-Merge一致性地优于多个强基线方法。

Conclusion: 可引导的模型合并策略能有效缓解源模型间的冲突，通过自适应调节机制可显著提升多语言推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Model merging is an effective technique for composing the capabilities of a multilingual model and a reasoning model. It has achieved promising generalization in multilingual reasoning tasks by aligning feature spaces of different models. However, the merged single model often fails to address the conflicts between source models, leading to suboptimal performance. In other words, the one-size-fits-all merging strategy may not align with the characteristics of different inputs which may require prioritizing certain models over others. To this end, we propose a Steerable Model Merging (ST-Merge) framework to modulate the contribution of each source model. To realize this idea, we introduce a gated cross-attention mechanism to weight or filter the two attended source models in an adaptive manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ST-Merge consistently outperforms multiple strong baselines on four multilingual reasoning benchmarks across 21 different languages.

</details>


### [46] [Sumi: Open Uniform Diffusion Language Model from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19005)
*Mengyu Ye,Keito Kudo,Wataru Ikeda,Ryosuke Matsuda,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Jun Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Sumi是一个完全开源、从头预训练的70亿参数均匀扩散语言模型，在1.5T token上训练，性能与相同token预算的自回归模型在知识、推理和编码基准上竞争力相当，但在常识基准上表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前自回归建模和掩码扩散建模已有大规模可用模型，但均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）缺乏从头预训练的大规模模型。为提供一个干净的参考点来研究扩展行为、生成动态、可控性以及与现有模型之间的权衡，作者开发了Sumi。

Method: 从头预训练了一个7B参数的均匀扩散语言模型Sumi，在1.5T token上训练，采用公开可用的教育密集型数据混合，并完全开源模型权重、检查点和完整训练方案。

Result: Sumi在知识、推理和编码基准上与相同token预算的自回归模型表现相当；在常识基准上表现不足，教育密集型数据混合可能是原因之一。

Conclusion: Sumi作为首个大规模从头预训练的均匀扩散语言模型，为社区研究均匀扩散机制提供了基础，并有望推动对其尚不充分理解方面的探索。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Among these, uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) permit any token to be updated at any step, in principle enabling more flexible generation. However, no UDLM has yet been pretrained from scratch at both large parameter scale and large token budget. Both autoregressive modeling and masked diffusion modeling already have capable models at scale that the community can study and build on; uniform diffusion has none. A scratch-pretrained UDLM at scale would provide a clean reference point for studying scaling behavior, generation dynamics, controllability, and trade-offs against established autoregressive and masked diffusion models. To this end, we introduce Sumi ("ink" in Japanese), a fully open 7B uniform diffusion language model pretrained from scratch on 1.5T tokens. Sumi performs competitively with autoregressive models trained at comparable token budgets on knowledge, reasoning, and coding benchmarks, while under-performing on commonsense benchmarks, where our education-heavy data mixture is a likely contributor. We release our model weights, checkpoints, and full training recipe, including a complete specification of the data mixture over publicly available corpora. We hope this release enables the community to study native uniform diffusion at scale and catalyzes work on its as-yet poorly understood aspects.

</details>


### [47] [Which Sections of a Research Paper Best Reveal Its Research Methods? Evidence from Library and Information Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19051)
*Qiuyu Fang,Jiayi Hao,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于全文物理位置的分段组合策略，用于研究方法的自动多标签分类。通过分析1,954篇图情领域论文，发现方法论信息在全文中的分布不均匀，中后段和末尾段判别力最强，结合书目元数据与跨段组合策略可有效提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究方法分类主要依赖标题和摘要，但摘要提供的方法信息有限；而利用全文则面临篇幅过长、信息冗余等挑战。因此需要探索更有效的全文分段利用策略。

Method: 提出基于物理位置的分段组合策略，将全文按物理位置划分。使用三个图情期刊（JASIST、LISR、JDoc）的1,954篇全文标注语料，评估不同段落及组合在多种模型上的分类表现。

Result: 方法论信息在全文中分布不均匀，中后段和末尾段判别力更强；结合书目元数据与跨段组合策略可有效提升分类性能。

Conclusion: 基于物理位置的分段组合策略能有效解决全文过长和信息冗余问题，提升研究方法多标签分类的准确率，为方法检索、综述生成和科研情报分析提供支持。

Abstract: Research methods are essential carriers of knowledge contribution in academic papers. Automatic multi-label classification of research methods can support knowledge services such as method retrieval, review generation, and research intelligence analysis. While existing studies primarily rely on titles and abstracts, abstracts often provide only limited methodological information, whereas utilizing full-text content faces challenges related to excessive length and information redundancy. Therefore, this paper proposes a segment combination strategy by partitioning the full-text content according to its physical postion. Using an annotated corpus of 1,954 full-text articles from three representative journals in Library and Information Science (JASIST, LISR, and JDoc), we evaluate the classification performance of various segments and their combinations across multiple models. Experimental results indicate that methodological information is distributed unevenly within the full-text content, with the middle-to-late and final segments exhibiting greater discriminative power. Furthermore, integrating bibliographic metadata with cross-segment combination strategies effectively enhances classification performance.

</details>


### [48] [Leadership as Coordination Control: Behavioral Signatures and the Recovery-Advantage Boundary in Multi-Agent LLM Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19111)
*Haewoon Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文借鉴团队科学中的领导力权变理论，研究多智能体LLM团队中过程级协调控制的有效条件。通过将三种经典领导风格（交易型、变革型、情境型）操作化为共享动作词汇上的控制器，发现没有控制器在所有任务组合上主导准确率，仅当第0轮多数投票不可靠、任务可恢复且无引导交互无法自行修复时，控制器才有增益。这表明过程级协调控制是一种需测量和理论映射的权变因素，而非可优化的排行榜指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队科学认为领导力是权变的——仅在特定条件下有效，而能力强、自主性高的团队可能根本不需要领导。作者将这一视角迁移到多智能体LLM团队，探究在什么可测量的条件下，过程级协调控制（即对智能体交互过程施加显式协调）能够带来价值增量。

Method: 使用行为特征（多数锁定、探索、从错误第0轮共识恢复）和逐动作消融实验，将交易型、变革型、情境型三种领导风格操作为共享动作词汇（探索、修订、接受、综合）上的控制器。匹配控制器使用相同动作但任意规则，以区分是理论驱动规则还是动作词汇起作用。在4种任务体制、3种开放权重模型家族下进行系统比较。

Result: 没有控制器在所有12个（模型，任务）组合上主导准确率。交易型控制与第0轮多数投票在所有组合上的差异在1.3pp以内。唯一增益出现在llama-4-scout social组合（第0轮多数不可靠），情境型控制比扁平交互高出8pp。恢复优势假设通过4个边界探测得到验证：控制器仅在第0轮多数不可靠、任务可恢复、且无引导交互无法自行修复时优于普通交互。

Conclusion: 过程级协调控制应被视为一种需要测量和理论映射的权变因素，而非一个需要刷榜的指标。本文的大体零结果恰恰是权变理论所预测的，而非控制器的失败。

Abstract: Team science holds that leadership is contingent: it helps only under specific conditions, and capable, autonomous teams may need none at all. We ask the analogous question for multi-agent LLM teams: under what measurable conditions does process-level coordination control add value, and do those conditions match what team science predicts? We use behavioral signatures (majority lock-in, exploration, recovery from an incorrect round-0 consensus) and per-action ablations, clean because each controller is an explicit action set, not a monolithic prompt. We operationalize three classical leadership styles (transactional, transformational, situational) as controllers over a shared action vocabulary (explore, revise, accept, synthesize). A matched controller with the same actions but an arbitrary rule recovers no better than majority voting, so the theory-derived rule, not the vocabulary, does the work. Across four task regimes and three open-weight model families, no controller dominates by accuracy, as the contingency view predicts: transactional control matches a shared round-0 vote on all 12 (model, regime) combinations to within 1.3pp, and gains appear only on the one combination where the round-0 majority is unreliable (llama-4-scout social; situational +8pp over flat). A recovery-advantage account, tested with four boundary probes, says a controller beats plain interaction only where the round-0 majority is unreliable, the task is recoverable, and undirected interaction does not already repair it. These regions map onto contingency theory (leadership substitutes, path-goal redundancy, the situational readiness gap), so a largely null accuracy result is what the theory predicts, not a failure of the controllers. We read process-level coordination control as a contingency to be measured and theory-mapped, not a leaderboard to be topped.

</details>


### [49] [Dango: A Strictly L1-Only Large Language Model for Studying Second Language Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19170)
*Shiho Matta,Yin Jou Huang,Fei Cheng,Takashi Kodama,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yugo Murawaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个1.8B参数的日语-英语第二语言习得大语言模型Dango，通过过滤预训练语料中的L2污染并微调于LLM生成的二语学习课程，实现了类似人类的L2产出模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLA研究多基于小型或非解码器模型，限制了开放文本生成能力和作为L2模拟器的实用性。需要更大规模的解码器模型来更好地模拟二语习得过程。

Method: 1) 提出过滤方法减少预训练语料中过早的英语暴露，同时保留真实的最小暴露；2) 在LLM生成的二语学习课程上进行微调，模拟L2习得过程。

Result: Dango发展了类似人类的L2产出模式，在性能上优于未过滤和多语言基线模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为可复现的计算SLA研究和面向学习者的应用提供了模型、数据和代码资源。

Abstract: We introduce Dango, a 1.8B-parameter large language model designed for controlled studies of L1-to-L2 (Japanese-to-English) transfer in second language acquisition (SLA). While previous studies have explored SLA in language models, they have predominantly relied on smaller or non-decoder models, limiting their ability to generate open-ended text and reducing their suitability as practical L2 simulators. We identify a key challenge when scaling models to this size: L2 contamination within the "monolingual" pretraining corpus used for L1 acquisition. To address this, we propose a filtering method to reduce premature exposure to English while preserving realistic, minimal exposure. We then fine-tune the model on LLM-generated L2-learning lessons to simulate the L2 acquisition process. Our evaluations confirm that Dango develops human-like L2 production patterns, outperforming both unfiltered and standard multilingual baselines. We release the model, data, and code to facilitate reproducible computational SLA research and learner-facing applications.

</details>


### [50] [Language Models as Interfaces, Not Oracles: A Hybrid LLM-ML System for Pediatric Appendicitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19183)
*Soheyl Bateni,Maryam Abdolali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClaMPAPP提出了一种混合系统架构，将LLM作为接口层从自由文本中提取结构化临床特征，再交由XGBoost分类器进行阑尾炎风险预测，在两个独立儿科队列中取得了优于端到端LLM的诊断性能，且对输入顺序扰动更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM直接作为诊断引擎存在对提示词敏感、对信息顺序敏感以及输出看似合理但错误的结果等局限；而结构化机器学习模型虽然预测更稳定，但需要难以融入叙事性临床工作流的表格化输入。因此需要一种既能利用自然语言输入的便利性，又能保持稳定诊断性能的混合方案。

Method: 提出ClaMPAPP系统，包含三个步骤：(1) 使用LLM从类笔记式叙事文本中提取符合模式约束的临床特征；(2) 应用确定性合理性检查；(3) 将验证后的特征输入经临床、实验室和超声变量训练的XGBoost分类器。评估在两个独立儿科阑尾炎队列上进行，对比包括开源和闭源模型的端到端LLM基线；通过模板渲染和约束LLM重写从结构化EHR生成叙事文本以保留真实标签，并引入句子顺序排列测试位置鲁棒性。

Result: ClaMPAPP在内部和外部验证中均取得最强的整体诊断性能，同时最小化漏诊阑尾炎病例（急性分诊中的关键安全指标）。端到端LLM表现出不稳定的敏感度-特异度权衡，且在叙事文本重排序下性能退化更严重。

Conclusion: LLM-as-interface、ML-as-predictor的设计能够分离自然语言可用性与预测推理功能，为临床决策支持提供了一条更可审计的路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can make clinical decision support more accessible by interpreting free-text documentation, but their direct use as diagnostic engines is limited by sensitivity to prompts, information order, and plausible but incorrect outputs. Structured machine-learning models offer more stable risk prediction, yet they require tabular inputs that are difficult to integrate with narrative clinical workflows. We present ClaMPAPP (Clinical Language-assisted Machine-learning Pipeline for Appendicitis), a hybrid system that uses an LLM as an interface rather than as the final decision-maker. ClaMPAPP extracts schema-constrained clinical features from note-like narratives, applies deterministic plausibility checks, and passes validated features to an XGBoost classifier trained on clinical, laboratory, and ultrasound variables. We evaluated ClaMPAPP on two independent pediatric appendicitis cohorts from German hospitals and compared it with end-to-end LLM baselines, including open-source and proprietary models. To preserve ground truth while testing free-text input, narratives were generated from structured electronic health records through template rendering and constrained LLM rewriting, with additional sentence-order permutation to assess positional robustness. ClaMPAPP achieved the strongest overall diagnostic performance in both internal and external validation while minimizing missed appendicitis cases, the key safety concern in acute triage. End-to-end LLMs showed unstable sensitivity-specificity trade-offs and greater degradation under narrative reordering. These results support an LLM-as-interface, ML-as-predictor design that separates natural-language usability from predictive inference and provides a more auditable pathway for clinical decision support.

</details>


### [51] [RECOM: A Validity Discrimination Tradeoff in Automatic Metrics for Open Ended Reddit Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19218)
*Pushwitha Krishnappa,Amit Das,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Tathagata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现现有自动评估指标在开放型、意见驱动的问答场景中面临有效性与判别力之间的权衡：余弦相似度能区分真实回答与随机噪声（Cohen's d ≈ 2），但无法区分模型优劣（|d| < 0.1）；BERTScore Precision看似能区分模型（|d| ≤ 0.63），但控制回答长度后判别力消失（|d| = 0.09），且有效性较弱（d ≈ 0.8）。作者提出RECOM数据集（15,000条Reddit问答，时间戳晚于所有模型训练截止日期），并建议在双轴上报告指标，同时引入随机基线作为基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示现有自动评估指标在开放型、意见驱动问答任务中面临的基本矛盾：同一个指标被要求同时完成两项任务——判断内容对齐是否真实（有效性）以及区分系统优劣（判别力），但这两项目标在开放式场景中相互冲突，且现有研究未系统量化这一权衡。

Method: 构建RECOM数据集（15,000条r/AskReddit问答，2025年9月，时间戳晚于所有被评模型的训练截止日期以确保无污染）；使用随机排列（random derangement）生成噪声基线；对五个7-10B开源LLM的输出，采用余弦相似度、BERTScore等多种指标进行评估，并控制回答长度等混淆因素；同时引入三个独立的LLM裁判进行人工评估对比。

Result: 余弦相似度在有效性上表现优秀（d ≈ 2），但在判别力上近乎为零（|d| < 0.1）；BERTScore Precision在未控制长度时判别力尚可（|d| ≤ 0.63），但控制长度后判别力崩塌（|d| = 0.09），且有效性较弱（d ≈ 0.8）。三个LLM裁判同样复制了有效性差距，且对五个模型的区分能力也很弱。该权衡是指标本身的属性，而非模型的属性。

Conclusion: 现有自动评估指标在开放型问答中无法同时兼顾有效性和判别力，建议在双轴（有效性与判别力）上报告指标，并引入显式的随机基线作为比较基准。RECOM数据集已公开发布。

Abstract: Automatic metrics are the default for evaluating LLM-generated text, yet a metric is quietly asked to do two jobs: tell genuine content alignment from surface coincidence (validity), and tell a better system from a worse one (discriminative power). On open-ended, opinion-driven question answering, the two are in tension. We introduce RECOM (Reddit Evaluation for Correspondence of Models), a contamination-free evaluation dataset of 15,000 r/AskReddit questions (September 2025), each paired with its authentic community replies, which postdate every evaluated model's training cutoff. Scoring five open-source LLMs (7--10B) against every reply each metric paired with a random-derangement noise floor we find that no metric does both jobs well. Cosine similarity separates real from random answers (Cohen's $d \approx 2$) but cannot rank the five models ($|d| < 0.1$); BERTScore precision appears to rank the models (raw $|d|$ up to 0.63), but once response length is controlled this collapses to $|d| = 0.09$ and its validity is weak ($d \approx 0.8$, versus cosine's $\approx 2$). Because every metric scores the same outputs, this validity--discrimination tradeoff is a property of the metrics, not the models, and we argue it stems from representation design. Three independent LLM judges reproduce the validity gap and likewise separate the five models only weakly. We recommend reporting metrics on both axes, with an explicit random-baseline floor. RECOM is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/recom-D4B0

</details>


### [52] [DreamReasoner-8B: Block-Size Curriculum Learning for Diffusion Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19257)
*Zirui Wu,Lin Zheng,Jiacheng Ye,Shansan Gong,Xueliang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Wei Bi,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamReasoner-8B，一种基于块扩散的语言推理模型。研究发现大块尺寸训练会导致推理性能显著下降，而小块尺寸则能保持有效性。为此提出块尺寸课程学习策略，逐步从细粒度过渡到粗粒度训练，从而弥合粒度差距。该模型在数学和代码推理基准上达到与Qwen3-8B等领先开放自回归模型竞争的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽能通过并行块状去噪加速解码，但其是否能够可靠地扩展到长思维链推理任务尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索块扩散模型在长COT推理中的可扩展性。

Method: 1) 开发DreamReasoner-8B开源块扩散推理模型；2) 系统研究训练和推理时块大小对长COT推理的影响；3) 提出块尺寸课程学习，从细粒度（小块）逐步过渡到粗粒度（大块）训练。

Result: 大块尺寸训练导致推理性能显著下降，小块尺寸能保持有效推理；块尺寸课程学习克服了粒度差距，在不同推理块大小下均表现出强泛化能力；DreamReasoner-8B在数学和代码推理基准上达到与Qwen3-8B竞争的水平。

Conclusion: 本研究为高效且具备推理能力的扩散语言模型奠定了实用基础。块尺寸课程学习是弥合训练与推理间粒度差距的有效策略，使扩散模型在长COT推理任务中具备竞争力。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models accelerate decoding through parallel block-wise denoising, yet whether they can be reliably scaled for long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning remains unresolved. To this end, we develop DreamReasoner-8B, an open-source block diffusion reasoning model, and conduct a systematic study of how training and inference block sizes affect long-CoT reasoning. Our analysis reveals a stark performance disparity: training with large block sizes yields remarkably poor reasoning, whereas small block sizes preserve effective reasoning. To bridge this granularity gap, we propose block-size curriculum learning, which gradually transitions training from fine-grained to coarse-grained block sizes, thereby overcoming this limitation and enabling strong reasoning performance that generalizes across diverse inference block sizes. On mathematical and code reasoning benchmarks, DreamReasoner-8B achieves results competitive with leading open autoregressive models such as Qwen3-8B. This work establishes a practical foundation for efficient, reasoning-capable diffusion language models. We release our model at https://github.com/DreamLM/DreamReasoner.

</details>


### [53] [Trade-offs in Medical LLM Adaptation: An Empirical Study in French QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19266)
*Ikram Belmadani,Oumaima El Khettari,Carlos Ramisch,Frederic Bechet,Richard Dufour,Benoit Favre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以法语医学问答为案例，系统比较了持续预训练(CPT)、监督微调(SFT)及其组合在不同模型族、规模和初始化类型下的领域适应效果。结果表明：对于多项选择问答，CPT+SFT组合通常最优但提升很小且常不显著，SFT是强且成本效益高的默认选择；对于开放式问答，CPT持续改善基于重叠的指标，而SFT常降低生成质量，指令微调和CPT+SFT在基于LLM的评估中更受青睐。跨语言实验显示法语适应可有效迁移到英语基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在专业领域（医学）和特定语言（法语）下的领域适应策略效果，厘清不同适应方法（CPT、SFT及其组合）与基础模型选择对性能的影响，为实际应用中的适应策略选择提供指导。

Method: 以法语医学问答为案例，比较CPT、SFT及CPT+SFT三种适应策略，涵盖三个模型族、多种模型规模和三种初始化类型。采用多项选择问答和开放式问答两种评估形式，在贪婪解码和约束解码下使用自动指标和LLM-as-a-Judge进行评估，并进行了跨语言迁移实验。

Result: MCQA中CPT+SFT最优但提升小且常不显著；OEQA中CPT改善重叠指标而SFT常降低生成质量；指令微调和CPT+SFT在LLM评估中更优；法语适应可有效迁移至英语基准。

Conclusion: SFT是多项选择问答中成本效益高的强默认选择；CPT对开放式问答的生成质量有益；提供了在计算约束下选择适应策略的实用指南。

Abstract: The development of large language models (LLMs) has led to an increased focus on their adaptation to specialized domains and languages, yet the effectiveness of domain adaptation strategies remains unclear. We present a study of medical domain adaptation using French medical question-answering (QA) as a case study. We compare continual pretraining (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and their combination across three model families, multiple sizes, and three initialization types, explicitly disentangling adaptation effects from base model choice. We evaluate both multiple-choice (MCQA) and open-ended QA (OEQA) under greedy and constrained decoding using automatic metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. For MCQA, CPT+SFT most often achieves the best scores, but gains over SFT are small and frequently not statistically significant, making SFT a strong and cost-effective default. For OEQA, CPT consistently improves overlap-based metrics, while SFT often degrades generation quality; instruction tuning and CPT+SFT are preferred by LLM-based evaluation. Cross-lingual experiments further show effective transfer from French adaptation to English benchmarks. Overall, we provide practical guidelines for selecting adaptation strategies under computational constraints.

</details>


### [54] [Enhancing Decision-Making with Large Language Models through Multi-Agent Fictitious Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19308)
*Leyang Shen,Yang Zhang,Xiaoyan Zhao,Chun Kai Ling,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多智能体虚拟博弈（MAFP），一种基于博弈论的新范式，通过将利益相关者立场建模为智能体并以均衡寻求过程解决决策任务中的"立场纠缠"问题，在锦标赛强度和鲁棒性上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统在可分解的执行任务中表现良好，但无法处理现实世界中广泛存在的决策任务——这些任务涉及多个利益相关者，其决策相互依赖、无法孤立求解。这种"立场纠缠"是一种不同于执行复杂性的决策复杂性，现有分而治之的范式无法应对。

Method: 提出MAFP范式，借鉴博弈论中虚拟博弈的原理：将各利益相关者的立场表示为独立智能体，每个智能体通过最佳响应其他智能体历史决策的经验混合来迭代更新自身决策，逐步逼近均衡解。

Result: 在竞争性决策任务上的实验表明，MAFP在锦标赛强度和鲁棒性两个互补指标上均优于单轮和多轮基线方法。

Conclusion: MAFP通过迭代博弈过程使智能体能够暴露并解决彼此的弱点，逐步提升决策质量和鲁棒性，有效解决了立场纠缠这一决策复杂性挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated great potential in solving tasks with execution complexity, by distributing subtasks across cooperative agents. However, this divide-and-conquer paradigm falls short on decision-making tasks that are also prevalent in the real world. These tasks require simultaneous reasoning from the stances of all involved stakeholders whose decisions are mutually dependent and thus cannot be solved in isolation. We characterize this challenge as stance entanglement, a form of decision complexity distinct from execution complexity. To address it, we propose Multi-Agent Fictitious Play (MAFP), a novel MAS paradigm that represents stakeholder stances as agents and formulates decision-making as an equilibrium-seeking process. Built on the game-theoretic principle of fictitious play, MAFP iteratively updates each agent's decision by best responding to the empirical mixture of other agents' past decisions. This enables agents to expose and address one another's weaknesses, progressively improving decision quality and robustness. We evaluate MAFP on challenging decision-making tasks that test the capability of deciding strategies for competitive scenarios prior to acting. MAFP outperforms both single-round and multi-round baselines on two complementary metrics, tournament strength and robustness, demonstrating its effectiveness in addressing stance entanglement.

</details>


### [55] [Freeing the Law with LOCUS: A Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19334)
*Denis Peskoff,Joe Barrow,Christopher Vu,Diag Davenport*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LOCUS是一个涵盖美国9,239个市县的地方法规语料库，通过OCR技术将碎片化的地方法规文本转化为机器可读格式，并提供了覆盖2,309个县的标准化访问层，以及基于ModernBERT的分类器和评分器，用于分析地方法规的透明度与家长主义等维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国地方法规（市政和县法规）是日常监管的核心，包括区划、住房、商业许可、公共卫生等领域，但这些法规分散在多个供应商平台，仅供人工浏览而缺乏批量研究访问，导致法律AI研究无法规模化利用这一重要法律层级。

Method: 1) 构建覆盖9,239个市县的地方法规原始语料库；2) 创建覆盖美国3,144个县中前2,309个（覆盖多数人口）的县级标准化访问层；3) 使用OCR技术处理多种文档格式；4) 发布带有覆盖元数据的语料库；5) 训练基于ModernBERT的分类器和评分器，用于分析法规的透明度与家长主义等维度。

Result: 原始语料库包含来自9,239个城市和县的法规代码；县级标准化层覆盖2,309个县，占美国多数人口；成功通过OCR处理了多种文档格式；发布了支持可复现性和增量扩展的覆盖元数据；训练了ModernBERT基分类器和评分器。

Conclusion: LOCUS填补了美国法律AI研究中地方法规层级的空白，为研究者提供了大规模机器可读的地方法律文本资源，支持法律AI研究的可复现性，并能够以前所未有的规模分析地方法规的透明度与家长主义等维度。

Abstract: Progress in legal AI increasingly depends on access to authoritative legal text at scale. Yet one of the most consequential layers of American law remains largely absent from existing machine-readable corpora: local ordinances. Local codes govern zoning, housing, business licensing, public health, noise, animal control, and many other domains of everyday regulation, but they are fragmented across vendor platforms designed for human browsing rather than bulk research access. We introduce LOCUS - the Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States - a comprehensive corpus and county-harmonized access layer for U.S. municipal and county ordinance codes. The raw corpus, available for release to researchers, represents nearly all publicly available municipal and county ordinance codes. The resulting raw corpus contains codes from 9,239 cities and counties. A smaller county-harmonized LOCUS access layer provides coverage for the largest 2,309 of 3,144 U.S. counties, accounting for a majority of the population. We use OCR to handle the myriad of document formats that have kept the law from being a public resource. We release the corpus with coverage metadata to support reproducibility, downstream legal AI research, and the incremental expansion of machine-readable access to local law. We train a collection of ModernBERT-based classifiers and scorers to facilitate analyzing U.S. local law among several dimensions, such as opacity and paternalism, that have not previously been studied at this scale. LOCUS-v1 and its derivative models are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LocalLaws/LOCUS-v1

</details>


### [56] [Learning User Simulators with Turing Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19336)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Cedegao E. Zhang,Linlu Qiu,Zexue He,Pengyuan Li,Alex Pentland,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Turing-RL方法，通过基于图灵测试的强化学习训练用户模拟器，使LLM生成的响应与真实用户难以区分，在对话和论坛讨论领域优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟方法通常训练LLM匹配单一真实响应（通过最大似然或相似度奖励），这种方法未能充分捕捉用户行为的真实性和多样性，导致模拟效果有限。

Method: 提出Turing-RL框架：使用判别式图灵奖励（由LLM评判者评估生成响应与真实用户响应的不可区分性），用户模拟器LLM通过强化学习优化该奖励，学习产生与真实用户无法区分的响应。

Result: 在对话聊天和Reddit论坛讨论两个领域，Turing-RL在LLM评估和人工评估指标上均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 优化响应的不可区分性（而非响应匹配）是训练用户模拟器的有效策略。

Abstract: Learning to simulate human users in interactive settings could advance the training of agent assistants, evaluation of personalization systems, research in the social sciences, and more. Existing approaches generally do so by training a large language model (LLM) to match a single ground truth response, either by maximizing the log probability or by using a similarity reward. We instead propose {Turing-RL}: a Turing-Test-based reinforcement learning approach for training user simulator models. {Turing-RL} uses a discriminative Turing reward with an LLM judge to score how indistinguishable a generated response is from the real user's given the user's history, and the user simulator LLM learns to produce responses indistinguishable from what the user could have said with such rewards. Across two different domains--conversational chat and Reddit forum discussion--we find that {Turing-RL} consistently outperforms baseline methods on both LLM and human evaluation metrics. Our study suggests that optimizing for indistinguishability, rather than response matching, is effective for learning user simulators.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [57] [Gaussian Mixture Attention: Linear-Time Sequence Mixing via Probabilistic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18283)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯混合注意力（GMA），一种概率性的注意力机制替代方案，通过K个高斯混合组分进行隐式路由，将标准点积注意力的O(N²)复杂度降至O(NK)，在长上下文分类任务上表现 competitive，因果版本超越线性/随机特征注意力但落后于优化后的SDPA和Mamba。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力的密集token间交互模式导致O(N²)复杂度，成为Transformer架构扩展到长上下文的核心瓶颈。

Method: 将查询和键映射到K个高斯混合组分的后验责任向量上，通过责任向量的重叠定义隐式亲和度，值通过K槽潜存记忆读写。利用矩阵乘法结合律避免显式构建N×N亲和矩阵，存储复杂度为O(NK)。提出双向和因果变体，提供端到端可微参数化方案。

Result: GMA实现预期的固定K线性内存缩放；在长上下文分类上与注意力基线相当；因果GMA在WikiText-103上优于线性/随机特征注意力变体，但落后于优化因果SDPA和Mamba。学习到的责任向量展现出广泛的组分使用和与表层词类别的适度对齐。

Conclusion: GMA是一种概率性、可解释、固定K线性时间的注意力风格替代方案，而非优化softmax注意力或状态空间模型的通用替代品。

Abstract: The dense token-to-token interaction pattern of standard dot-product attention remains a central bottleneck in scaling Transformer architectures to long contexts. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Mixture Attention (GMA)}, a probabilistic attention-style sequence mixer that replaces explicit pairwise query--key comparison with routing through $K$ learned Gaussian mixture components. Queries and keys are mapped to posterior \textit{responsibility} vectors over a shared latent routing space; their overlap defines an implicit responsibility-space affinity, while values are written into and read from a $K$-slot latent memory. By exploiting the associativity of matrix multiplication, GMA avoids materializing the induced $N\times N$ affinity matrix and instead uses two responsibility matrices whose dominant activation storage scales as $\mathcal{O}(NK)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ for fixed $K$. We formulate bidirectional and causal variants of GMA, provide an end-to-end differentiable parameterization of the Gaussian mixture components, and analyze its responsibility-modulated gradient structure, constrained non-negative low-rank affinity interpretation, and local routing stability. Empirically, GMA exhibits the intended fixed-$K$ linear memory scaling and is competitive with attention-style baselines on long-context classification, while causal GMA improves over tested linear/random-feature attention variants on WikiText-103 but remains behind optimized causal SDPA and Mamba in the current implementation. Analysis of learned responsibilities further shows broad component usage and moderate alignment with surface-form token categories, supporting GMA as a probabilistic, interpretable, fixed-$K$ linear-time attention-style alternative rather than a universal replacement for optimized softmax attention or state-space models.

</details>


### [58] [Breaking the Solver Bottleneck: Training Task Generators at the Learnable Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18284)
*Lorenz Wolf,Connor Watts,Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew Daborn-Sargent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROPEL提出一种求解器摊销框架，通过轻量级激活探针预测任务通过率，从而无需在每个候选任务上执行完整求解器回滚即可训练任务生成器，显著提升强化学习中前沿任务供给的效率。在数学、代码和软件工程任务上，该方法将可学习前沿任务的比例提升约2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练智能体的瓶颈正日益变为前沿任务供给——即有效、可解且难度恰好适应当前模型的任务。固定任务分布会随模型提升而饱和，而朴素合成生成的任务往往过于简单、不可解或不适定。使用RL训练任务生成器以优化有效性和可学习性可以解决这一瓶颈，但直接优化需要对每个候选任务进行重复求解器回滚，在软件工程任务中单次回滚可能耗时数十分钟，导致求解器在环的生成器训练难以实现。

Method: PROPEL框架包括：(1) 在一次性标注的任务生成与求解器结果语料上训练轻量级激活探针；(2) 该探针从冻结的生成器参考模型中预测目标求解器的通过率；(3) 在生成器优化过程中，探针作为通过率的代理，将生成器评估简化为单次前向传播，避免昂贵的求解器回滚。

Result: 在多个模型规模上：对于编程任务，可学习前沿任务比例从10.1%提升至20.0%（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct求解器），从5.3%提升至12.6%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct求解器）；对于软件工程任务，在探测器和生成器训练未见过的仓库上，面向目标求解率的前沿任务占比从9.8%提升至19.6%（Qwen3.5-27B求解器）。

Conclusion: PROPEL通过求解器摊销策略有效解决了RL任务生成器训练中求解器回滚成本过高的问题，能够将生成分布有效地转向目标求解率，在数学、代码和软件工程任务上均取得显著提升，且具备良好的跨模型规模泛化能力。

Abstract: The limiting resource for training agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly frontier task supply: valid, solvable tasks just difficult enough to train the current model. As reasoning and agentic models improve, fixed task distributions saturate, while naive synthetic generation yields tasks that are trivial, impossible, or ill-posed. Training a task generator with RL to optimize validity and learnability can address this bottleneck, but direct optimization requires repeated solver rollouts per candidate. For software-engineering (SWE) tasks, a single rollout can take tens of minutes; solver-in-the-loop generator training is intractable. We introduce PROPEL, a solver-amortized framework for training task generators at the targeted solve rate. PROPEL trains a lightweight activation probe on a one-time labeled corpus of generated tasks and solver outcomes. The probe predicts target-solver pass rate from a frozen generator reference model and serves as a proxy for solve rate during generator optimization, reducing generator evaluation to a single forward pass. Across math, code, and software-engineering at multiple model scales, PROPEL shifts generation toward the targeted solve rate: for coding, tasks generated at the learnable frontier increase from $10.1\% \rightarrow 20.0\%$ for a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct solver and from $5.3\% \rightarrow 12.6\%$ for a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct solver. For SWE, PROPEL increases the share of generations at the targeted solve rate from $9.8\% \rightarrow 19.6\%$ for Qwen3.5-27B on repositories not seen during training of probe and generator.

</details>


### [59] [CODEBLOCK: Learning to Supervise Code at the Right Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18286)
*Zhijie Deng,Ling Li,Jinlong Pang,Kaiqin Hu,Qi Xuan,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeBlock提出了一种结构感知的稀疏监督框架，通过选择完整的代码块而非孤立token进行监督，在仅使用1.9%的监督token情况下，在六个代码生成基准测试中取得了优于全token SFT的pass@1表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码语言模型的监督微调通常对所有响应token使用统一的交叉熵损失，假设每个token提供同等有用的学习信号。自然语言SFT中token级选择方法虽挑战了这一假设，但直接将token级掩码迁移到代码领域会破坏语法和语义完整的程序单元，因为代码依赖于结构完整性和定义-使用关系。

Method: CodeBlock框架包括：1) 选择高质量的指令-响应对；2) 将代码响应划分为语法连贯的编码项（coding items）；3) 通过聚合核心逻辑token上的广义交叉熵估计编码项效用；4) 使用数据流可达信号和桥接信号对编码项重排序，优先选择传播或连接重要程序依赖的块；5) 训练时完整响应保留作为上下文，但损失仅应用于选中的代码项和有信息量的自然语言token。

Result: 在六个代码生成基准测试上，CodeBlock取得了比全token SFT更强的平均pass@1，同时仅使用1.9%的监督响应token。

Conclusion: 结构感知的稀疏监督策略能够有效提升代码LLM微调效率，证明代码领域需要保留结构完整性的选择性监督，而非简单迁移自然语言的token级掩码方法。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of code LLMs typically applies uniform cross-entropy loss to all response tokens, implicitly assuming that every token provides equally useful learning signal. Recent token-level selection methods challenge this assumption in natural-language SFT by supervising only high-value tokens. However, directly transferring token-level masking to code can break syntactically and semantically coherent program units, because code depends on structural completeness and definition-use relations. We therefore propose CodeBlock, a structure-aware sparse supervision framework that selects structure-complete code evidence rather than isolated tokens. CodeBlock first selects high-quality instruction-response pairs, then partitions code responses into syntactically coherent coding items, estimates their utility by aggregating generalized cross-entropy over core logic tokens, and reranks them with data-flow reach and bridge signals to prioritize blocks that propagate or connect important program dependencies. During training, the full response remains available as context, while loss is applied only to selected code items and informative natural-language tokens. Experiments on six code-generation benchmarks show that CodeBlock achieves stronger average pass@1 than full-token SFT and competitive selection baselines, while using only 1.9% of supervised response tokens.

</details>


### [60] [Artemis: Anatomy-Resolved inTervention for Eliminating Multimodal NeuroImage confounderS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18287)
*Siyuan Dai,Yang Du,Kun Zhao,Zhusuyi Chen,Heng Huang,Paul Thompson,Chao Shi,Haoteng Tang,Liang Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Artemis，一个区域级因果框架，通过在独立脑区层面学习特定混杂因素表征并进行因果干预，消除人口统计学因素对多模态脑网络分析的混杂影响，作为即插即用模块可提升任意GNN骨干在脑疾病诊断等任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经影像结合fMRI功能连接与DTI结构连接，使用GNN分析脑网络时，年龄、性别等人口统计学因素会系统性混淆脑连接与临床结果之间的关系，导致GNN利用虚假捷径而非学习因果不变表征。现有因果GNN方法未考虑临床神经影像数据中真实的混杂因素，且脑网络基于图谱分区构建，各区域对人口统计学因素敏感度不同，需要区域感知的调整。

Method: 提出Artemis，一个区域级因果框架，在每个独立脑区层面学习轻量参数的特定混杂因素表征，进行因果干预（后门调整），并综合多模态功能与结构特征进行图推理，作为即插即用模块兼容任意GNN骨干。

Result: 在ADNI疾病诊断、OASIS痴呆分期和HCP性别分类三个基准数据集上，Artemis一致性地优于代表性GNN基线方法，多项支持性实验进一步证明了统计显著性和神经科学可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过区域级别的因果干预有效消除了人口统计学混杂因素的影响，提升了GNN在脑网络分析中的因果不变性和泛化性能，具有神经科学可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging, integrating functional connectivity from fMRI and structural connectivity from DTI, enables non-invasive analysis of brain networks using graph neural networks. However, demographic factors such as age and sex systematically confound the relationship between brain connectivity and clinical outcomes, causing GNNs to exploit spurious shortcuts rather than learning causally invariant representations. While recent causal GNN methods introduce causality at the graph-modeling level, their causal mechanisms remain domain-agnostic without accounting for the real-world confounders inherent in clinical neuroimaging data. Moreover, brain networks are constructed from atlas-based parcellations where each region exhibits distinct sensitivity to demographic factors, necessitating region-aware adjustment. We propose Artemis, a region-level causal framework that bridges this gap with causal intervention at each brain region independently by learning region-specific confounder representations with lightweight parameters. Our adjustment comprehensively utilized the multimodal functional and structural features for graph reasoning as a plug-in module compatible with arbitrary GNN backbones. Experiments on three benchmarks, ADNI for disease diagnosis, OASIS for dementia staging, and HCP for sex classification, demonstrate consistent improvements over representative GNN-based baselines. Multiple supporting experiments further demonstrate statistical significance and neuroscientific interpretability.

</details>


### [61] [A Link between Shock-wave Theory and Symmetry-reduced Stochastic Gradient Descent for Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18303)
*Taiki Miyagawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了冲击波理论与随机梯度下降对称商化学习动力学之间的数学联系，通过微分几何、李群理论和流体力学，证明了在商流形上有效动力学满足粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程，且粗粒化损失函数的梯度服从Burgers型方程，可严格证明激波形成。该理论适用于MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络，并提出对称修正的商可观测量可作为监控、预测和控制训练相变的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习训练动力学中存在对称性冗余导致参数观测失真问题，缺乏严格的数学框架来统一理解随机梯度下降过程中的相变和激波现象。作者希望建立一种数学显式的方法，将冲击波理论与深度学习训练动力学联系起来，为监控和预测训练动态提供理论依据。

Method: 利用微分几何、李群理论和流体力学工具，对参数对称性进行商化处理并应用局部熵粗粒化，推导出商流形上的粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程；在假设原始参数动力学可被商空间上的梯度场概括的条件下，证明粗粒化损失函数的梯度服从Burgers型方程，严格建立激波形成条件。

Result: 该理论成功应用于MLP、CNN、Transformer和平均场网络，验证了这些架构均满足Hamilton-Jacobi或Burgers型方程。揭示了原始参数范数因对称性冗余而失真，而对称修正的商可观测量能提供更可靠的训练相变监控依据。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅建立了冲击波理论与深度学习训练动力学之间的严格数学联系，还为实际深度学习训练提供了对称修正的诊断工具，有望用于预测和控制训练过程中的相变行为。

Abstract: We develop a mathematically explicit link between shock-wave theory and the symmetry-quotiented learning dynamics of stochastic gradient descent, drawing on differential geometry, Lie group theory, and fluid mechanics. Specifically, after quotienting parameter symmetries and applying local-entropy coarse-graining, the effective dynamics satisfy a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation on the quotient manifold. Moreover, under the assumption that the raw parameter dynamics can be summarized by a gradient field on the quotiented space, the gradient of the coarse-grained loss function obeys a Burgers-type equation, and shock formation can be established rigorously. We apply our theory to multilayer perceptrons, convolutional neural networks, Transformers, and mean-field networks, and show that they obey the Hamilton--Jacobi or Burgers-type equations. We conjecture that this framework also yields practical diagnostics for deep learning. In architectures such as Transformers, raw parameter norms are often distorted by symmetry redundancy and may therefore be misleading, whereas symmetry-corrected quotient observables provide a principled basis for monitoring, forecasting, and controlling training-phase transitions.

</details>


### [62] [Attribution-Guided and Coverage-Maximized Pruning for Structural MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18304)
*Yifu Ding,Jiacheng Wang,Ge Yang,Yongcheng Jing,Jinyang Guo,Xianglong Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向MoE模型的结构化剪枝框架，通过将剪枝比率分配建模为通道得分覆盖率最大化问题，并利用基于归因的方法高效求解，在DeepSeek和Qwen MoE模型上实现了50%或25%结构化剪枝（结合4-bit量化）下保持模型精度，在Qwen3-30B-A3B上实现了5.27倍的内存压缩，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型压缩方法主要在专家级别操作（移除整个专家或基于粗粒度重要性排序），这种专家级决策过于粗糙，无法捕捉细粒度冗余，导致剪枝预算分配不当和压缩效果有限。

Method: 观察到MoE专家中的信息高度集中在少量通道上，即使被认为是重要的专家也存在大量冗余。基于此，提出将剪枝比率分配形式化为通道得分覆盖率最大化问题，并使用基于归因的近似方法高效求解，实现面向MoE模型的结构化剪枝框架。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen MoE模型上，50%或25%结构化剪枝结合4-bit量化后仍保持模型精度；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上实现5.27倍内存压缩，在多个基准测试中一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的通道级结构化剪枝方法比专家级剪枝更精细高效，能够有效利用MoE模型中的通道冗余实现大幅压缩，同时保持模型性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale compute efficiently, yet remain expensive to deploy due to their substantial memory footprint and inference overhead. Prior compression methods mainly operate at the expert level, either removing entire experts or ranking experts by coarse-grained importance scores. However, such expert-wise decisions are often too coarse to capture fine-grained redundancy, leading to misallocated pruning budgets and limited compression. To address this problem, we observe that information within MoE experts is highly concentrated in a small subset of channels, leaving substantial redundancy even in experts deemed important. Based on this observation, we propose a structural pruning framework tailored for MoE models. Our method reformulates prune-ratio allocation as a channel-score coverage maximization problem and solves it efficiently using an attribution-based approximation. Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen MoE models show that our method preserves model accuracy under 50% or 25% structured pruning when combined with 4-bit quantization. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, our approach reduces memory footprint by 5.27$\times$ and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [Fisher Width: A Geometric Measure of Complexity on Statistical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18306)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher宽度，作为统计流形上高斯宽度的类比，用Fisher信息度量替代欧几里得度量来度量统计模型的几何复杂度，并证明了其在浓度性、扰动稳定性等方面的性质及泛化界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯宽度是欧几里得空间中衡量集合有效维度的核心概念，但统计模型本质上是黎曼流形（由Fisher信息度量诱导），欧几里得度量无法捕捉统计可区分性带来的各向异性几何效应。需要一种能适应统计流形曲率且具有重参数化不变性的复杂度度量。

Method: 在参数点θ处，用局部度量张量G(θ)^{1/2}替换欧几里得恒等映射，定义Fisher宽度为Fisher重缩放后的集合的高斯宽度。发展了Fisher宽度的基本理论，包括浓度不等式、度量扰动稳定性、与欧几里得基线的谱比较界等，并提出了可计算估计器。

Result: Fisher宽度保留了高斯宽度的关键结构特征（浓度性、扰动稳定性等），同时能捕捉欧几里得度量无法体现的各向异性几何效应。在MNIST上的三个模型类别上进行了实证评估。

Conclusion: Fisher宽度是统计流形上高斯宽度的自然类比，为研究弯曲统计流形上的复杂性和学习问题奠定了基础，并给出了Fisher-Lipschitz假设类的泛化界。

Abstract: Gaussian width is a central geometric complexity measure in high-dimensional probability, compressed sensing, convex optimization, and learning theory. It quantifies the average extent of a set along random directions, thereby capturing the effective dimension of constraint sets, hypothesis classes, and descent cones. However, this notion is intrinsically Euclidean. Statistical models instead carry a natural Riemannian geometry induced by the Fisher information metric, where directions are scaled according to statistical distinguishability rather than ambient Euclidean length.
  We introduce Fisher width, a Fisher-geometric analogue of Gaussian width for statistical manifolds. At a parameter point $θ$, Fisher width replaces the Euclidean identity by the local metric tensor $G(θ)^{1/2}$, measuring the Gaussian width of the Fisher-rescaled set. This makes the resulting quantity sensitive to local statistical curvature and invariant under smooth reparameterizations.
  We develop the basic theory of Fisher width, showing that it retains key structural features of Gaussian width, including concentration, metric perturbation stability, and spectral comparison bounds with the Euclidean baseline, while also capturing anisotropic geometric effects invisible to Euclidean measures. As an application, we prove a generalization bound for Fisher-Lipschitz hypothesis classes and propose computable estimators, which we evaluate empirically on MNIST across three model classes.
  Fisher width is to statistical manifolds what Gaussian width is to Euclidean convex bodies. This work lays the foundation for studying complexity and learning on curved statistical manifolds.

</details>


### [64] [DRIFT: Refining Instruction Data via On-Policy Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18307)
*Zefan Wang,Lincheng Li,Tianyu Yu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种基于on-policy影响函数的数据精炼方法，通过解决标准影响函数的近端差距和梯度范数偏差问题，显著提升SFT阶段LLM的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选方法在有限预算下能加速训练，但难以提升模型能力上限。需要一种方法能从数据分布层面精选出最能提升最终模型性能的实例。

Method: 提出DRIFT框架：(1) 使用模型on-policy rollout作为验证目标，缓解近端差距；(2) 基于轨迹正确性进行符号加权；(3) 对影响分数进行梯度黑客偏差去偏，使少量验证查询可作为可靠锚点。

Result: 在7B参数的指令微调和推理模型上，DRIFT持续提升了性能上限，优于现有数据筛选基线方法。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过解决IF的结构性局限，有效优化了SFT数据分布，能够突破模型的能力上限。

Abstract: Optimizing the training data distribution for Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) dictates the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). While existing data curation methods excel at accelerating training under constrained budgets, they are less suited to elevating the capability upper bound. The challenge here is no longer to identify a smaller subset that preserves performance, but to refine the data distribution toward instances most capable of improving the final model. To address this problem, we explore instance-level data attribution using Influence Functions (IF). We identify that standard IF formulations struggle in this setting due to two structural limitations: a proximity gap caused by off-policy validation targets, and a severe bias towards gradient norm. We propose DRIFT (Data Refinement via On-Policy Influence Functions for Supervised Fine-Tuning). Instead of relying on external reference data, DRIFT utilizes the model's on-policy rollouts as validation targets, which empirically minimizes the parameter proximity gap and better aligns with the local neighborhood assumption of IF. It further applies signed weighting based on trajectory correctness and debiases influence scores against the gradient hacking issue, allowing a small set of validation queries to act as reliable anchors for attributing the full dataset. Experiments on 7B-parameter instruction and reasoning models show that DRIFT consistently raises the performance ceiling on both, outperforming existing data curation baselines.

</details>


### [65] [TRIDENT: Breaking the Hybrid-Safety-Physics Coupling for Provably Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18308)
*Zijie Meng,Ziwei Li,Yufei Liu,Zhiyu Li,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhua Nie,Bingcai Wei,Miao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIDENT框架，通过协同设计三个组件（Richardson-Romberg梯度校正、Lyapunov约束顺序信任域更新、物理信息残差评论家），解决了网络化CPS中安全强化学习面临的混合离散-连续动作、硬安全约束和物理驱动动力学的三重耦合偏差问题，实现了O~(1/√K)收敛到约束纳什均衡和O(√K)累计违规界，在多个基准上将训练违规降低95.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化信息物理系统中的安全协调要求学习算法同时处理混合离散-连续动作、训练时的硬安全约束以及物理驱动的动力学，这三个特征形成了有向偏差循环，任何现成模块的简单组合都无法解决这一三重耦合问题。

Method: TRIDENT框架包含三个协同设计的组件：1) Richardson-Romberg梯度校正，将Gumbel-Softmax偏差从O(τ)降至O(τ²)；2) Lyapunov约束顺序信任域更新，保证每次迭代的可行性；3) 物理信息残差评论家，分解值函数而非奖励。三者共同设计以消除耦合泄漏。

Result: 在多无人机移动边缘计算、自主交叉口管理和混合SMAC变体上，TRIDENT相比MADDPG降低95.5%的训练违规，相比MACPO降低76.3%，同时相比最强无约束基线提升13.5%的奖励。理论证明了O~(1/√K)的收敛率和O(√K)的累计违规界。

Conclusion: TRIDENT通过协同设计三个核心组件有效解决了安全MARL中的三重耦合难题，在理论和实验上均验证了其在保障安全性的同时保持高奖励的有效性。

Abstract: Safe coordination in networked cyber-physical systems forces learning algorithms to simultaneously handle hybrid discrete-continuous actions, hard training-time safety constraints, and physics-governed dynamics. We show that these three features form a directed cycle of biases that defeats any naive composition of off-the-shelf modules, and formalize this as a three-way coupling lemma. We then introduce TRIDENT, the first MARL framework whose three components are co-designed to cancel each leak: a Richardson-Romberg gradient correction reducing Gumbel-Softmax bias from O(tau) to O(tau^2), a Lyapunov-constrained sequential trust-region update enforcing per-iterate feasibility, and a physics-informed residual critic that decomposes value rather than reward. We prove an O~(1/sqrt(K)) convergence rate to a constrained Nash equilibrium and an O(sqrt(K)) cumulative-violation bound. On multi-UAV mobile-edge computing, autonomous intersection management, and a hybrid SMAC variant, TRIDENT cuts training-time violations by 95.5% over MADDPG and 76.3% over MACPO, while improving reward by 13.5% over the strongest unconstrained baseline.

</details>


### [66] [SAGE: Retain-Aware Post-Hoc Sanitization of Final Unlearning Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18309)
*Jingyuan Zhang,Yucheng Bai,Peixi Wen,Zhehao Huang,Zhengbao He,Hanling Tian,Xinwen Cheng,Haiyin Ran,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE方法，一种后置的、源无关的修正技术，用于净化LLM遗忘过程中的最终更新向量，通过抑制与保留知识方向对齐的成分来缓解遗忘与保留之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法都存在遗忘效果与保留能力之间的权衡，且无法在不重新运行遗忘流程的情况下修复对保留能力的损害。

Method: 提出SAGE（Spectral Activation-GEometry Sanitization），一种后置修正方法：从小量保留代理数据中收集真实模块激活，提取其主导激活几何结构，通过闭式求解源锚定优化目标，抑制与高能保留方向对齐的更新成分，同时保留源方法的遗忘载体。

Result: 跨多种遗忘方法、模型规模和基准测试，SAGE一致地缓解了保留-遗忘权衡，表明最终向量的后置净化是实用且未被充分探索的机器遗忘研究轴。

Conclusion: 保留激活偏差可用于量化遗忘方法对保留能力的损害，且后置净化（如SAGE）无需重跑原始遗忘流程即可恢复保留性能，为机器遗忘提供了新的研究维度。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning aims to remove undesirable knowledge or behaviors while preserving retained capabilities. Current unlearning methods all involve a trade-off between unlearning and retention. We have found that the retention activation bias can also be used to quantify the damage an unlearning method inflicts on retention, without considering the specific implementation of the unlearning process. This allows us to restore retention performance for any unlearning method using a post-hoc approach. Therefore, we propose a complementary post-hoc setting to sanitize the final update vector without rerunning the original unlearning pipeline. In this setting, we design SAGE, Spectral Activation-GEometry Sanitization, a source-agnostic correction for final unlearning updates. SAGE collects real module inputs from a small retain proxy, extracts their dominant activation geometry, and solves a source-anchored optimization objective in closed form, which suppresses update components aligned with high-energy retained directions while preserving the source method's forgetting carrier. Across multiple unlearning methods, model scales, and benchmarks, SAGE consistently relieves the retain-forget trade-off, identifying post-hoc sanitization of final vectors as a practical and underexplored axis for machine unlearning.

</details>


### [67] [Ghost Attractor Networks: Basin-Structured Dynamical Decoders for Closed-Loop Sequential Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18315)
*Tianyu Wang,Ying Wang,Zhihao Liu,Xi Vincent Wang,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ghost Attractor Networks，一种基于势能漂移的理论驱动动力学解码器，其隐空间具有盆地-吸引子结构，在机器人动作解码任务中，以2.3M参数匹敌1.07B参数的扩散Transformer（462倍参数减少，32倍延迟降低），并在LIBERO-10闭环基准上达到95.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型Transformer和扩散解码器在序列输出生成中存在随序列长度增长的内存成本和迭代逐步骤计算开销；而小型前馈解码器虽恢复效率，但产生无结构的隐表示，限制了闭环控制（如相位条件化动作生成和跨步骤隐变量传递需要稳定的盆地几何结构）。

Method: 从理论上推导了具有势能漂移的动力学解码器，其隐空间通过构造产生盆地-吸引子结构。三个设计目标（多模态、解码器级单步切换、恒定内存）驱动势能-漂移形式，模式转变通过鞍结分岔和鬼吸引子逃逸实现。采用层次化相空间分解，将一阶盆地收敛与二阶本体感受精炼分离。使用行为克隆与对比学习目标进行端到端训练。

Result: 在1430个保留样本上，势能的梯度范数在5个积分步骤内衰减67%。2.3M参数的Ghost在离线MSE上以462倍参数减少和32倍延迟降低匹敌1.07B参数的扩散Transformer，并优于五种2M参数基线（MLP、神经ODE、CVAE、Transformer、1步扩散）5.9%-29%。在LIBERO-10闭环基准上，相位条件化带来13.5%的成功率提升，持久隐变量集成达到95.7%的最终成功率。

Conclusion: Ghost Attractor Networks通过理论驱动的盆地-吸引子隐空间设计，实现了高效率与强闭环控制能力的统一，验证了结构化隐空间在机器人动作解码中的关键价值。

Abstract: Sequential output generation with large-scale Transformer and diffusion decoders pays a memory cost that grows with sequence length, plus iterative per-step computation. Replacing them with small feed-forward decoders restores efficiency but produces unstructured latent representations that limit closed-loop control: phase-conditioned action generation and cross-step latent carry-over both require a latent geometry with stable basins. This article proposes Ghost Attractor Networks, a theoretically derived dynamical decoder whose latent evolves under a learned potential with drift and produces a basin-attractor structure by construction. Three desiderata (multi-modality, decoder-level single-pass switching, and constant memory) motivate the potential-drift form, and mode transitions arise as saddle-node bifurcations with ghost-attractor escape. A hierarchical phase-space decomposition separates first-order basin convergence from second-order proprioceptive refinement. Empirically, a Ghost trained end-to-end with a behavioral-cloning and contrastive objective exhibits the predicted gradient-flow contraction in its potential, with the gradient norm decaying by 67 percent across five integration steps on 1430 held-out samples. Ghost is evaluated as a robotic action decoder. A 2.3-million-parameter Ghost matches the offline accuracy of a 1.07-billion-parameter Diffusion Transformer at 462 times fewer parameters and 32 times lower latency, and beats five alternative 2M-parameter decoders (MLP, Neural ODE, CVAE, Transformer, 1-step Diffusion) on offline mean squared error by 5.9 to 29 percent. On the LIBERO-10 closed-loop benchmark, phase conditioning on Ghost's basin-structured latent yields a 13.5 percentage-point success-rate gain over a feed-forward MLP baseline, and persistent-latent ensembling reaches a 95.7 percent final success rate.

</details>


### [68] [A Survey on Data-Driven Models for Soil Moisture Regression and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18316)
*Ilektra Tsimpidi,George Georgoulas,Vidya Sumathy,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于AI的土壤湿度建模方法，将现有方法分为五类：统计时间序列模型、地统计方法、经典机器学习、深度学习和概率/贝叶斯方法，利用多源环境数据进行回归或分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理模型计算成本高、可扩展性有限，亟需灵活的数据驱动方法来解决土壤湿度建模这一复杂的时空学习问题。

Method: 对AI-based SM模型进行结构化综述，将现有方法组织为五个类别：统计时间序列模型、地统计方法、经典机器学习、深度学习和概率/贝叶斯方法。

Result: 提出了五类AI模型的分类框架，明确了各类模型利用的数据源（历史SM记录、气象变量、植被指数、地形、土壤特征、地理位置信息）及任务类型（回归/分类）。

Conclusion: AI方法作为物理模型的灵活替代方案，可有效处理土壤湿度建模中的非线性环境交互和异构数据源问题。

Abstract: Soil Moisture (SM) modelling constitutes a complex spatiotemporal learning problem characterised by nonlinear environmental interactions, heterogeneous data sources, and limited ground observations. Physics-based approaches, such as water balance models, rely on explicit hydrological equations and high-quality inputs, but their computational cost and scalability limitations restrict large-scale deployment. Data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) methods have emerged as flexible alternatives, enabling the extraction of empirical relationships between soil moisture and environmental variables with reduced modelling assumptions. This work presents a structured survey of AI-based models for soil moisture estimation and classification. Existing approaches are organized into five categories: (a) statistical time-series models, (b) geostatistical methods (c) classical machine learning (ML) models, (d) Deep Learning (DL) models and (e) Probabilistic/Bayesian methods. These models leverage historical soil moisture records, meteorological variables, vegetation indices, topography, soil characteristics, and geolocation data to perform regression or classification tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Enhanced Graph Neural Networks using K-Hop Gaussian Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18317)
*Xuling Zhang,Peng Wang,Daiyan Li,Aoran Huang,Zeiwei Chen,Yongkui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出K-Hop高斯扩散核（KHG）作为图神经网络预处理模块，通过多跳扩散与高斯加权平衡局部与全局信息传播，在噪声或结构复杂图上优于传统消息传递GNN及现有扩散核方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN依赖单跳消息传递，受限于边噪声和局部邻域；现有PPR和Heat Kernel扩散核虽实现全局传播，但仍难以处理复杂局部结构和远距离节点噪声。

Method: 设计KHG扩散核作为图数据预处理模块，采用多跳扩散机制并对远程节点施加高斯权重，在应用标准GNN之前完成局部与全局信息的平衡传播。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，KHG显著优于传统消息传递GNN以及PPR和Heat Kernel扩散核，尤其在噪声或结构复杂的图上表现突出。

Conclusion: KHG扩散核有效解决了GNN中边噪声和远程节点信息传播的瓶颈问题，为图数据预处理提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Most graph neural network (GNN) cores rely on graph convolutions, typically implemented as message passing between direct (single-hop) neighbors. In many real-world graphs, edges can be noisy or poorly defined, limiting information propagation to local neighborhoods. Existing diffusion kernels, such as Personalized PageRank (PPR) and Heat Kernel, alleviate this issue through global propagation, but still struggle with complex local structures and distant node noise. To address these limitations, we propose a K-Hop Gaussian (KHG) diffusion kernel as a preprocessing module for graph data. KHG introduces multi-hop diffusion with Gaussian weighting for remote nodes, balancing local and global information propagation before applying standard GNNs. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that KHG significantly outperforms traditional message-passing GNNs, as well as PPR and Heat Kernel diffusion, particularly in noisy or structurally complex graphs.

</details>


### [70] [ASTRA: A Scalable Next-Generation ATCO Training Simulator with Autonomous Simpilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18319)
*Ethan Chew,Enjia Wu,Iruss Eng Wei Yeow,Ian Weiqin Lim,Ranen Sim,Brandon Koh Ziheng,Kaleb Nim,Caden Toh Jun Yi,Wei Dong Soin,Darius Kai Keat Koh,Galen King Yu Tay,Prannaya Gupta,Jonathan Ee Fang Koong,Yong Zhi Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASTRA是一个端到端训练模拟器，通过自动化模拟飞行员和空管角色、使用本地化语音模型（将WER从107.80%降至23.45%）以及AI辅助评估框架（在准确性、简洁性和完整性上分别达到91.7%、88.2%和86.9%），解决了新加坡空管培训中依赖人工模拟员和语音模型不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空管培训依赖专业人工模拟员（simpilots），培训容量受限；现有自动化方案使用西方中心语音模型，在新加坡口音航空语音上表现极差（WER高达107.80%），无法满足本地化运营需求。

Method: 构建ASTRA端到端训练模拟器：1) 转录空管语音并解析指令；2) 使用本地化语音模型自动生成飞行员和空管响应；3) 集成AI辅助评估框架，从准确性、简洁性和完整性三个维度评估受训者通信质量。

Result: 微调ASR管道将WER降至23.45%，显著优于现有方案；AI评估框架在准确性、简洁性和完整性上分别达到91.7%、88.2%和86.9%。

Conclusion: 基于DSPy和Unsloth等开源基础构建的ASTRA方法，能够实现可扩展、标准化的空管评估，同时减少教员工作负担。

Abstract: Air Traffic Control Operators (ATCOs) are vital in ensuring the safe, orderly, and efficient flow of air traffic, yet training capacity is constrained by reliance on specialized human trainers known as simpilots, who must role-play both pilots and ATCOs in a simulated airspace. Existing automated solutions rely on Western-centric speech models that perform poorly in Singaporean operational contexts, with off-the-shelf systems exhibiting Word Error Rates (WER) of up to 107.80% on Singaporean-accented aviation speech. We introduce ASTRA, an end-to-end training simulator that automates these simpilot roles through a pipeline that transcribes ATCO speech, interprets instructions, and generates appropriate pilot and ATCO responses using locally adapted voice models. Our fine-tuned Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) pipeline reduces WER to 23.45%, substantially outperforming existing approaches in this domain. Beyond traffic simulation, ASTRA incorporates an AI-assisted performance evaluation framework that assesses trainee radiotelephony communications across accuracy, brevity, and completeness, achieving post-optimization scores of 91.7%, 88.2%, and 86.9%, respectively. Built on open-source foundations such as DSPy and Unsloth, this approach enables scalable, standardized ATCO assessment while reducing instructor workload.

</details>


### [71] [SAE Interventions are Unreliable: Post-Intervention Recovery of Suppressed Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18322)
*Mingyue Cui,Linghui Shen,Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏自编码器（SAE）在潜在空间防御中存在一个可恢复的失败模式：即使成功钳制了特定的“不安全”特征，模型行为仍可通过残差扰动恢复。实验表明，在拒绝性引导等安全关键场景中，恢复率高达95.8%，而目标特征相对漂移仅为0.131。恢复路径分析将这种现象归因于SAE重建残差——即SAE未解释的残差分量。这揭示了特征级控制与行为完整性之间的差距：控制SAE特征并不保证控制底层行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在空间防御方法依赖SAE分解出的“不安全”特征作为监控和干预的句柄，假设钳制这些特征就能可靠地阻止模型不良行为。作者质疑这一假设的有效性，试图揭示并验证这种干预可能存在的可恢复性漏洞。

Method: 将问题形式化为“干预后恢复”（post-intervention recovery）的约束残差空间优化问题。从干预后的残差状态出发，优化残差扰动以恢复干预前行为，同时保持目标SAE特征的干预后值不变。使用编码器正交更新（单层）和特征映射雅可比矩阵（跨层）确保恢复不会简单地撤销干预。

Result: 在TPP、unlearning、IOI和拒绝性引导四个实验中均观察到可恢复行为。在拒绝性引导中，有效样本恢复率达95.8%，被防御特征的相对漂移仅为0.131，显著低于基于后缀的基线方法。恢复路径分析定位到SAE重建残差是恢复行为的主要来源。

Conclusion: SAE特征支持因果干预，但控制这些特征并不保证控制底层行为。特征级控制与行为完整性之间存在根本性差距，潜在空间防御需要更鲁棒的设计来弥补这一缺陷。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) decompose residual-stream activations into interpretable features. Recent latent-space defenses increasingly rely on these decompositions, assuming that identified "unsafe" SAE features serve as actionable handles for monitoring and intervention. In this paradigm, clamping a specific harmful feature is expected to reliably prevent model misbehavior. However, we show that this success may hide a recoverable failure mode: the clamp may block one visible route to a behavior without eliminating the behavior itself. We formulate this vulnerability as post-intervention recovery, a constrained residual-space optimization problem. Starting from the post-intervention residual state, we optimize residual perturbations to recover the pre-intervention behavior while preserving the post-intervention values of the targeted SAE features. Even under a strong threat model where the intervention remains active throughout optimization and generation, recovery remains possible.
