However, it is well-recognized that the lack of standardized model performance evaluation benchmarks makes fair comparison difficult and hinders further innovation, and thus benchmark standardization is in urgent need. Furthermore, many published glucose forecasting algorithms are limited to CGM data alone, ignoring other multimodal signals such as insulin dosing and carbohydrate intake. Here, we introduce MetaboNet-Bench, a benchmark for multimodal glucose forecasting for patients with type 1 diabetes that provides an extensible open-source evaluation framework for comparison of glucose forecasting algorithms that leverage glucose, insulin, and carbohydrate data. We then demonstrate its utility by benchmarking several recently published glucose forecasting models and a custom multimodal time-series model, representing different model architectures. The results show that the benefit of adding data modalities is conditioned on the complexity of the model and that incorporating more clinical metrics helps identify meaningful gaps to fill for future research.

</details>


### [108] [BLADE: Scalable Bi-level Adaptive Data Selection for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18650)
*Jiaxing Wang,Deping Xiang,Jin Xu,Zirui Liu,Zicheng Zhang,Guoqiang Gong,Jun Fang,Chao Liu,Pengzhang Liu,Tongxuan Liu,Ke Zhang,Qixia Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BLADE提出了一种无需Hessian矩阵的双层数据选择框架，通过拉格朗日乘子将基于影响力的方法转化为带惩罚的单层目标，用动态参考模型替代静态模型，理论保证一阶收敛，实验表明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据选择方法存在根本性局限：基于影响力的方法需要计算不可行的逆Hessian矩阵，而基于过量损失的方法虽计算高效但依赖静态参考模型，在训练过程中与不断演化的代理模型逐渐失配。

Method: BLADE通过拉格朗日乘子将双层优化问题重构为带惩罚的单层目标，避免逆Hessian计算；恢复出过量损失形式但用动态参考模型替代静态模型；理论证明一阶收敛性；实例化为无记忆随机块坐标Frank-Wolfe算法实现高效在线批次选择。

Result: 大量实验表明BLADE在数据选择上持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: BLADE提供了一个实用的LLM训练数据选择方案，在理论保证和实际效果上均具有优势。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) datasets scale to trillions of tokens, data selection has emerged as a critical frontier to filter out uninformative noise and construct adaptive learning trajectories. Beyond static heuristic filtering, advanced data selection methods for LLM training largely follow two paradigms, each with fundamental limitations. Influence-based methods provide principled bi-level objectives but require intractable inverse-Hessian computations, while excess-loss methods are computationally efficient but rely on a static reference model that becomes misaligned with the evolving proxy model during training. We propose BLADE (Bi-Level Adaptive Data sElection), a Hessian-free framework for data selection. BLADE reformulates the bi-level optimization problem underlying influence-based methods as a penalized single-level objective via Lagrange multipliers, avoiding inverse-Hessian computation while revealing a principled connection to excess-loss based data selection. The resulting objective recovers an excess-loss form but replaces the static reference model with a dynamic one that stays synchronized with training. Theoretically, we prove that this penalized formulation guarantees first-order convergence. For efficient online batch selection, we instantiate BLADE as a memoryless randomized block-coordinate Frank-Wolfe algorithm. Extensive experiments show that BLADE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art data selection baselines, providing a practical recipe for LLM training.

</details>


### [109] [scGTN: Deep Siamese Graph Transformer Network for Single-cell RNA Sequencing Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18672)
*Jinke Wu,Yifan Wang,Siyu Yi,Caiyang Yu,Ziyue Qiao,Nan Yin,Jiancheng Lv,Wei Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出scGTN，一种基于深度孪生图Transformer网络的单细胞RNA-seq聚类框架，通过构建图增强视图、整合最短路径信息与节点距离、以及最优传输自监督策略，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scRNA-seq聚类方法忽略了数据的稀疏性与噪声，以及细胞间复杂的结构信息。

Method: 1. 将scRNA-seq数据构建为图，生成两个增强图视图捕获互补的细胞间信息；2. 采用孪生图Transformer网络融入最短路径信息和节点距离，捕获更丰富的结构关系；3. 使用最优传输策略进行自监督聚类。

Result: 在多个基准scRNA-seq数据集上，scGTN一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的scGTN框架有效整合了基因表达谱与细胞间结构依赖关系，能够更准确地完成单细胞聚类任务。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) serves a pivotal role in characterizing gene expression at the cellular level, enabling the identification of cell types and advancing the understanding of cellular heterogeneity. Despite the significant progress in scRNA-seq data clustering, we argue that current methods always ignore the sparsity and noise, as well as the complex intercellular structural information inherent in scRNA-seq data. Toward this end, in this paper, we propose a novel single-cell RNA-seq clustering framework via deep Siamese Graph Transformer Network (termed scGTN), which explicitly integrates gene expression profile and intercellular structural dependencies for cell clustering. In particular, we formulate scRNA-seq data as a graph and construct two augmented graph views that serve as dual views to capture complementary intercellular information. Then, a Siamese graph transformer network is employed to explicitly incorporate shortest-path information and node-wise distances for capturing richer structural relationships between cells. Finally, we employ an optimal transport strategy to guide the cell clustering in a self-supervised manner. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark scRNA-seq datasets demonstrate that our scGTN consistently outperforms existing methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/W-RMSL/scGTN.

</details>


### [110] [InTrain: Intrinsic Trainability for Zero-Cost Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18676)
*Qinqin Zhou,Fuhai Chen,Jipeng Wu,Zhiwei Chen,Zhikai Hu,Weiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InTrain，一种统一的理论代理指标，通过几何容量（激活协方差特征谱的参与比）和优化韧性（累积梯度健康度）的乘法耦合来形式化神经架构的内在可训练性，在NAS基准上达到与集成方法相当的排序相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零成本代理指标依赖碎片化启发式方法，未能捕捉架构可训练性的本质问题，缺乏统一的理论基础。

Method: 提出InTrain指标，包含两个组件：1) 几何容量——通过激活协方差特征谱的参与比量化表示流形的有效维数；2) 优化韧性——通过累积梯度健康度评估跨深度反向传播的鲁棒性。两个组件通过尺度不变的乘法耦合进行整合。

Result: 在标准NAS基准和搜索空间上的实验表明，InTrain达到了与最先进的集成代理指标相当的排序相关性，并优于其他单一指标方法。

Conclusion: 可训练性可以通过几何容量与优化韧性的乘法耦合来形式化，为无需训练的网络架构搜索提供了统一的理论代理指标。

Abstract: Training-free neural architecture search promises efficient discovery of high-performance networks without costly training. However, existing zero-cost proxies rely on fragmented heuristics that fail to capture the fundamental question: what makes an architecture trainable? This paper introduces Intrinsic Trainability (InTrain), a unified theoretical proxy that formalizes trainability as an architectural invariant emerging from two synergistic components: geometric capacity and optimization resilience. We operationalize intrinsic trainability through analysis of neural information processing. Geometric capacity is quantified via the participation ratio of activation covariance eigenspectrum, capturing the effective dimensionality of representation manifolds. Optimization resilience is measured through cumulative gradient health, assessing the robustness of backpropagation across network depth. InTrain synthesizes these dimensions through a scale-invariant multiplicative coupling, which we hypothesize is essential for capturing their synergistic, non-additive relationship. Extensive experiments on standard NAS benchmarks and search spaces demonstrate that InTrain achieves ranking correlations on par with state-of-the-art ensemble-based proxies and outperforms other single-metric methods.

</details>


### [111] [Bounded Context Management for Tabular Foundation Models on Stream Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18677)
*Jinmo Lee,Doyun Choi,Moongi Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CURE策略，通过不确定性感知的准入和冗余感知的驱逐管理上下文，使表格基础模型在流式学习中超越传统方法达27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式表格学习面临分布漂移，传统方法通过更新模型状态适应，而表格基础模型(TFM)以上下文内方式预测，将挑战从更新模型转向管理上下文。需要一种有效的上下文管理策略。

Method: 提出CURE策略，包含两个核心机制：熵门控的上下文准入（不确定性感知）和冗余感知的上下文驱逐，根据未来信息视角的三个要求（保留近期样本、保留不确定样本、移除冗余样本）设计。

Result: 在7个流式数据上，CURE相比经典流式学习器提升最高27.0%，在多个TFM骨干上表现稳健，在策略变体中排名第一。

Conclusion: 将流式学习挑战从模型更新重新定义为上下文管理是有效的，CURE策略通过不确定性感知和冗余感知机制显著提升了表格基础模型在流式场景下的性能。

Abstract: Tabular stream learning requires predictions on sequentially arriving examples under distribution shift. While standard methods adapt by updating model states, tabular foundation models (TFMs) make predictions conditioned on a labeled context in an in-context manner, making them a natural alternative for stream learning. This shifts the challenge from how to update the model to how to manage the context. We propose a future information view that yields three practical requirements for context management: preserve recent examples, retain uncertain examples, and remove redundant examples. We instantiate these requirements as CURE (Context management via Uncertainty-aware admission and Redundancy aware Eviction), a context-managing policy with entropy-gated admission and redundancy-aware eviction. Across seven streams, CURE shows up to 27.0% relative improvement over classical stream learners, remains robust across multiple TFM backbones, and ranks first among other policy variants. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/morcellinus/CURE-ICML-FMSD.

</details>


### [112] [Dual-Channel Grounded World Modeling (DCGWM): Structural Prevention of Objective Interference Collapse via Heterogeneous External Grounding with Inward-Only Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18688)
*Akshay Hazare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在多模态世界模型学习中存在目标干扰坍缩（OIC）问题，即物理动态（稀疏、高幅值）与社会行为动态（弥散、分布匹配）两个通道的梯度竞争会导致表征子空间被主导通道系统性坍缩。提出双通道接地世界模型（DCGWM），通过分区潜在空间（物理子空间Z_p、行为子空间Z_b）和内向梯度流来结构性防止OIC，并给出三个理论结果证明该方案的有效性。（实验验证尚在进行中）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型在面对物理动态和社会行为动态两类异质外部信号时，由于共享潜在空间导致梯度干扰，发生目标干扰坍缩（OIC）——主导通道（物理）的梯度会系统性坍缩从属通道（社会行为）的表征子空间，且单纯调整损失权重无法解决。

Method: 提出DCGWM架构：(1) 分区潜在空间——物理子空间Z_p和社会行为子空间Z_b，采用内向梯度流（不跨子空间传播）；(2) 物理接地通道通过VICReg对齐物理测量值更新Z_p；(3) 社会行为接地通道通过对齐多智能体模拟轨迹更新Z_b；(4) 通道间接口模块在任务层面耦合子空间但不产生跨子空间梯度；(5) 非对称接地遵守损失——物理违规用硬铰链惩罚，行为偏移用软KL散度；(6) 生成渲染层与潜在世界模型架构隔离。

Result: 三个理论结果：(1) 分区消除了导致OIC的梯度干扰路径；(2) 每个接地子空间从其对齐目标继承了抗坍缩保证；(3) 在生成目标几何的假设下，生成隔离是必要的。实验验证正在进行中。

Conclusion: 本文建立了问题形式化和架构设计，通过结构性分区而非权重调参来解决多模态世界学习中的通道坍缩问题，但对方法的实验验证尚未完成，将在未来修订中报告。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are a leading approach to world model representation learning. We identify a failure mode in JEPA-based world models grounded against two qualitatively distinct external signals: physical dynamics (sparse, high-magnitude, constraint-satisfying gradient corrections) and social-behavioral dynamics (diffuse, distribution-matching corrections). We term this Objective Interference Collapse (OIC): we argue that joint learning in a shared latent space causes the dominant channel to systematically collapse the subordinate channel's representational subspace, in a manner not resolvable by loss weighting alone. We propose Dual-Channel Grounded World Modeling (DCGWM), designed to structurally prevent OIC through a partitioned latent space (physical subspace Z_p, behavioral subspace Z_b) with inward-only gradient flow. A Physical Grounding Channel updates only Z_p via VICReg-style alignment to physical measurements; a Social-Behavioral Grounding Channel updates only Z_b via alignment to trajectories from an emergent multi-agent simulation. An Inter-Channel Interface Module couples the subspaces at the task level without cross-subspace gradients. An Asymmetric Grounding Adherence Loss penalizes rollout drift with a hard hinge for physical violations and a soft KL for behavioral divergence. A Generative Rendering Layer is architecturally isolated from the latent world model. We present three theoretical results: the partition removes the gradient-interference pathway implicated in OIC; each grounded subspace inherits anti-collapse guarantees from its alignment objective; and generative isolation is necessary under a stated assumption on the generative objective's geometry. This manuscript establishes the problem formulation and architecture; experimental validation is ongoing and will be reported in a future revision.

</details>


### [113] [Robust and Interpretable Adaptation of Equivariant Materials Foundation Models via Sparsity-promoting Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18691)
*Youngwoo Cho,Seunghoon Yi,Wooil Yang,Sungmo Kang,Young-woo Son,Jaegul Choo,Joonseok Lee,Soo Kyung Kim,Hongkee Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种稀疏促进微调方法，通过利用E(3)等变材料基础模型的结构特性，仅更新约3%（最低0.5%）的参数，在全量微调和等变低秩适应方法上达到或超越性能，并在能量/力预测、磁矩预测等任务中展现通用性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的材料基础模型因物理化学多样性和计算设置不匹配，需要领域特定的校准，而现有的全量微调或低秩适应方法在效率和可解释性上存在不足。

Method: 提出一种稀疏促进微调方法，利用E(3)等变材料基础模型的结构特性，选择性地仅更新少量参数（约3%~0.5%），实现高效领域适配。

Result: 在分子和晶体基准的能量/力预测任务上，该方法以仅更新约3%（最低0.5%）的参数，匹配或超越全量微调和等变低秩适应方法的性能；并在磁矩预测和磁性总能量建模任务上验证了任务泛化能力；稀疏模式分析揭示了物理上可解释的特征，如过渡金属体系中增强的d轨道贡献。

Conclusion: 稀疏促进微调是一种灵活且可解释的方法，适用于等变材料基础模型的领域专业化。

Abstract: Pre-trained materials foundation models, or machine learning interatomic potentials, leverage general physicochemical knowledge to effectively approximate potential energy surfaces. However, they often require domain-specific calibration due to physicochemical diversity as well as mismatches between practical computational settings and those used in constructing the pre-training data. To address this, we propose a sparsity-promoting fine-tuning method that selectively updates model parameters by exploiting the structural properties of E(3)-equivariant materials foundation models. On energy and force prediction tasks across molecular and crystalline benchmarks, our method matches or surpasses full fine-tuning and equivariant low-rank adaptation while updating only $\sim$3~\% of parameters, and in some cases as little as $\sim$0.5~\%. Beyond energy and force calibration, we further demonstrate task generalizability by applying our method to magnetic moment prediction and magnetism-aware total energy modeling. Finally, analysis of sparsity patterns reveals physically interpretable signatures, such as enhanced $d$-orbital contributions in transition metal systems. Overall, our results establish sparsity-promoting fine-tuning as a flexible and interpretable method for domain specialization of equivariant materials foundation models.

</details>


### [114] [Attention as Frustrated Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18694)
*Joshua Nunley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出受挫同步网络（FSN），利用复相位和耦合核实现注意力机制，在百万参数级字符级文本和代码任务上，验证损失低于 tuned RoPE-SwiGLU Transformer，并收敛至1.5953。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全同步的振荡器网络无法进行进一步计算，因此基于同步的注意力架构必须在结构性的不同步中定位计算。作者希望构建一种利用同步受挫（frustrated synchronization）来实现注意力与下一词预测的神经网络。

Method: FSN将token状态表示为环面上的相位，整个值通路通过学习一个复数耦合核（含谐波和一步延迟）实现。耦合核的每个分量对应同步文献中的受挫：复相位为Kuramoto-Sakaguchi受挫角，符号谐波为Daido排斥分量，延迟项代数上等价于K-S耦合（受挫角由数据自身的转移决定），从而实现将下一词预测建模为被数据受挫的同步过程。

Result: 在百万参数和训练预算匹配的条件下，FSN在字符级文本和代码任务上的验证损失在每个epoch均低于tuned RoPE-SwiGLU Transformer；FSN在30个epoch后即可低于Transformer收敛的50 epoch损失1.611，50 epoch收敛至1.5953±0.0014。去除了所有MLP的变体（用平均场耦合替代）仍可追踪Transformer表现。在自然文本上，无受挫基座层在长程拷贝任务上落后于收敛的Transformer，而核反转变体在4层及以上弥补了这一差距。规模扩展至4百万参数时优势持续。

Conclusion: FSN作为一种基于受挫同步的注意力替代架构，在参数效率、收敛速度和最终损失上均优于同等规模的Transformer基线，验证了将同步受挫机制用于序列建模的有效性。

Abstract: A network of oscillators that synchronizes perfectly computes nothing further, so an attention architecture built from synchronization must locate its computation in structured departures from agreement. We introduce the Frustrated Synchronization Network (FSN), whose token states are phases on a torus and whose entire value pathway is one learned complex coupling kernel over harmonics and a one-step delay. Each component of the kernel is a frustration in the sense of the synchronization literature. The complex phases are static Kuramoto-Sakaguchi frustration angles, the signed harmonics are repulsive Daido components, and the delay term, which couples each token to the successors of the tokens it attends to, is algebraically identical to Kuramoto-Sakaguchi coupling whose frustration angle is the data's own transition, so next-token prediction is implemented as synchronization frustrated by the data. At matched one-million-parameter and training budgets on character-level text and code, the FSN's validation loss is below a tuned RoPE-SwiGLU transformer's at every epoch measured, and the comparison survives training the baseline to convergence: every thirty-epoch enwik8 seed finishes below the transformer's converged fifty-epoch loss of 1.611, and the FSN's completed fifty-epoch runs converge to 1.5953 +/- 0.0014. A variant with every feed-forward block replaced by mean-field coupling to learned collective modes, leaving no multilayer perceptron in the stack, tracks the transformer. On natural text the unfrustrated base layer falls behind the converged transformer at every copy depth, worst on long-range copy events; the kernel reverses the deficit at every depth of four and beyond. Headline comparisons are at the one-million-parameter scale; a scale ladder is complete through four million parameters with the advantage persisting, and remaining arms are marked as in progress.

</details>


### [115] [Graph Grounded Cross Attention Transformer Neural Network for Structurally Constrained Full Event Sequence Generation in Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18726)
*Fang Wang,Ernesto Damiani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GGATN模型，利用全局过程图作为结构化活动记忆，结合Transformer自注意力和图引导交叉注意力，一次性生成活动、时间戳、长度和属性，再通过Viterbi式图约束解码生成可行路径，在六个事件日志上优于LLM基线，实现零幻觉活动和零属性不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构约束下的事件序列生成面临保持转移可行性、时序顺序、终止条件和属性一致性等挑战。在预测性过程监控中，全事件序列生成任务尚未被充分解决，现有工作主要关注下一活动、剩余时间、结果和属性预测等子任务。

Method: 提出GGATN（Graph Grounded Cross Attention Transformer Neural Network），包含：全局过程图作为结构化活动记忆、Transformer自注意力进行序列位置上下文化、图引导交叉注意力注入过程拓扑结构。采用单次生成活动、时间戳、长度和事件级及序列级属性，随后通过Viterbi式图约束解码保证可行路径和显式终止。

Result: 在六个基准事件日志上，GGATN在序列相似度、Damerau-Levenshtein相似度、二元组控制流相似度和持续时间分布上表现优异，零幻觉活动、零序列级属性不一致。消融实验证实全局图编码器作为稳定的结构先验。

Conclusion: GGATN有效解决了结构约束下的事件序列生成问题，为预测性过程监控中的全事件序列生成提供统一解决方案，图结构、序列上下文、反馈修正和约束解码共同塑造生成过程。

Abstract: Structurally constrained event sequence generation remains challenging because generated paths must preserve transition feasibility, temporal order, termination, and attribute consistency. In predictive process monitoring (PPM), this challenge appears as full event sequence generation, whereas existing work mainly addresses component tasks such as next activity, remaining time, outcome, and attribute prediction. This paper proposes the Graph Grounded Cross Attention Transformer Neural Network (GGATN) for this unified PPM task. GGATN uses a global process graph as structured activity memory, contextualizes sequence positions through Transformer self attention, and injects process topology through graph grounded cross attention. Unlike autoregressive decoding, GGATN generates activities, timestamps, length, and event level and sequence level attributes in a single pass, followed by Viterbi style graph constrained decoding for feasible paths and explicit termination. Experiments on six benchmark event logs show more reliable generation quality than local instruction prompted LLM baselines. GGATN achieves strong performance on sequence similarity, Damerau Levenshtein similarity, bigram based control flow similarity, and duration distribution, while maintaining zero hallucinated activities and zero sequence level attribute inconsistency. Ablation analyses confirm the global graph encoder as a stable structural prior. Interpretability analyses show how graph structure, sequence context, feedback refinement, and constrained decoding shape generation.

</details>


### [116] [Stealthy World Model Manipulation via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18697)
*Yibin Hu,Xiaolin Sun,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SWAAP，首个针对学习世界模型的两阶段数据投毒框架。第一阶段通过双层优化识别有害目标世界模型，第二阶段通过梯度匹配实现隐蔽投毒，在连续控制任务中显著降低性能且能规避多种防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的学习代理使用习得的世界模型预测未来状态、规划动作和适应新环境。然而，从收集的经验更新世界模型的过程创造了一个训练时攻击面：对抗性污染的精调轨迹可以操纵习得的动力学，进而破坏下游规划。

Method: SWAAP包含两个阶段：(1) 通过过渡梯度定理实现的一阶双层优化，识别有害目标世界模型，使其在规划下产生低回报行为但保持接近清洁动力学；(2) 通过隐式约束梯度匹配实现目标，仅修改有限比例的精调过渡目标，使诱导的训练梯度将受害模型推向对抗目标，同时预测误差正则化器鼓励污染目标保持接近世界模型的自然逼近误差。

Result: 在多种连续控制任务中，SWAAP导致显著性能下降，同时保持污染过渡接近清洁数据，并规避了评估的非自适应残差/CUSUM/TRIM风格防御。

Conclusion: 揭示了世界模型适应管线中存在的实际漏洞，强调了需要保护世界模型训练数据和习得动力学的鲁棒性方法。

Abstract: Model-based learning agents use learned world models to predict future states, plan actions, and adapt to new environments. However, the process of updating world models from collected experience creates a training-time attack surface: adversarially poisoned fine-tuning trajectories can manipulate the learned dynamics and thereby corrupt downstream planning. In this paper, we propose SWAAP, the first two-stage data poisoning framework for learned world models. In the first stage, SWAAP identifies a harmful target world model that induces low-return behavior under planning while remaining close to clean dynamics, using first-order bilevel optimization enabled by a transition-gradient theorem. In the second stage, SWAAP realizes this target through stealth-constrained gradient matching, modifying only a limited fraction of fine-tuning transition targets so that the induced training gradients steer the victim model toward the adversarial target, while a prediction-error regularizer encourages the poisoned targets to remain close to the world model's natural approximation error. To assess attack stealthiness, we evaluate defenses and detectability across three stages of the poisoning pipeline: pre-training detection of poisoned transitions, robust training during fine-tuning, and test-time monitoring of the resulting world model. Across diverse continuous-control tasks, SWAAP causes substantial performance degradation while keeping poisoned transitions close to clean data and evading the evaluated non-adaptive residual/CUSUM/TRIM-style defenses. These results reveal a practical vulnerability in world-model adaptation pipelines and highlight the need for robustness methods that protect both world-model training data and learned dynamics.

</details>


### [117] [Private Learning with Public Feature Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18773)
*Shuli Jiang,Walid Krichene,Nicolas Mayoraz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cond-DP，一种在标签差分隐私（label DP）回归设置下利用公开特征矩阵结构来加速优化收敛的方法。通过引入数据驱动的条件矩阵重塑优化景观，Cond-DP在凸、强凸和非凸设定下均提供收敛保证，并在线性回归中实现比标准DPSGD更快的收敛速度，且不增加额外隐私成本。实验表明，Cond-DP在多种数据集和模型架构上 consistently 优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标签DP或半敏感特征的回归场景中，现有方法主要针对分类问题，回归任务的有效方法尚不充分。同时，公开特征（如推荐系统中的用户特征）往往具有快速衰减的谱结构，这一特性未被有效利用来提升隐私约束下的优化效率。

Method: 提出Cond-DP，一种条件化DPSGD变体，核心思想是利用公开特征矩阵构建数据驱动的条件矩阵（conditioning matrix），通过重塑优化景观来加速收敛。该条件矩阵可直接从公开特征构造，不消耗隐私预算。算法在凸、强凸和非凸设定下均给出了收敛性分析，且当条件矩阵退化为单位矩阵时，Cond-DP恢复为标准DPSGD。

Result: 理论方面，Cond-DP在标签DP线性回归中实现了比标准DPSGD更快的收敛速度，且不增加额外隐私成本。实验方面，Cond-DP在多种数据集和模型架构上 consistently 优于现有最先进基线，展现出强健且稳定的实际性能。

Conclusion: Cond-DP有效填补了标签DP回归场景下的方法空白，通过利用公开特征的结构信息显著提升隐私约束下的优化效率，为半敏感特征设置下的回归任务提供了理论完备且实践有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study differentially private (DP) regression in settings where each data sample includes public, non-sensitive features -- common in applications such as recommendation and advertising systems. While such label-DP or semi-sensitive-feature settings have been primarily explored in the context of classification, effective approaches for regression remain underexplored. We introduce Cond-DP, a conditioned variant of DPSGD that leverages the structure of public feature matrices to improve optimization under privacy constraints. Motivated by the observation that these public features often exhibit rapidly decaying spectra, Cond-DP incorporates a data-driven conditioning matrix to reshape the optimization landscape and accelerate convergence. We provide convergence guarantees for convex, strongly convex, and non-convex settings, and recover standard DPSGD as a special case when the conditioning matrix is the identity. We show how to construct an effective conditioning matrix for Cond-DP directly from public features, enabling provably faster convergence than DPSGD in private linear regression without incurring additional privacy cost. Empirically, Cond-DP with this conditioning matrix consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across a wide range of datasets and model architectures under label DP, demonstrating strong and robust performance in practice.

</details>


### [118] [Contextualizing Biological Language Models across Modalities via Logit-Space Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18703)
*Yanjun Shao,Yundi Chen,Yashvi Patel,Aurelien Pelissier,María Rodríguez Martínez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOGICA是一种在输出logit空间进行对比学习的框架，通过门控跨模态适配器保留预训练语言模型的token概率接口，实现上下文条件化预测，在蛋白质-配体结合、TCR-肽活性及药物耐药性预测等任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生物语言模型通过掩码token预测提供token级概率分布，但这些分布是从无标注语料学习的，无法自然适应特定任务的生物上下文（如互作伙伴、细胞环境、治疗干预）。现有上下文匹配方法通过池化嵌入、对比潜在空间或任务特定预测头等方式扭曲了这一概率接口。

Method: LOGICA在输出logit空间中执行对比学习，使用门控跨模态适配器（兼容各模型的原始token头），保留预训练似然接口，将上下文token对数似然转换为匹配分数。对齐基于上下文敏感的token概率而非共享嵌入空间中的距离，可在不同词汇表、无共享分词器或解码器的模型间从稀疏配对数据中学习。

Result: 在蛋白质-配体结合、TCR-肽活性和药物条件耐药性预测任务中，LOGICA超越了包括匹配潜在对比和条件MLM基线在内的现有最优方法。在保留基因的单突变药物耐药性预测中，AUC从潜在空间基线的~0.55提升至~0.65。

Conclusion: LOGICA通过保留token级接口实现可解释性和生成能力，在变异局部排序（将比较简化为扰动位点的条件突变token似然）中尤其有效。

Abstract: Pretrained biological language models expose per-token probability distributions through masked-token prediction, providing the likelihood interface central to sequence design, variant scoring, and mechanistic interpretation. Yet these distributions are learned from broad unlabeled corpora and are not naturally conditioned on task-specific biological contexts such as interaction partners, cellular environments, or therapeutic interventions. Existing contextual matching methods often distort this interface through pooled embeddings, contrastive latent spaces, or task-specific prediction heads. We introduce LOGICA (Logit-space Contrastive Alignment), a framework for context-conditioned prediction that performs contrastive learning directly in output-logit space. Using gated cross-modal adapters compatible with each model's native token head, LOGICA preserves the pretrained likelihood interface and converts contextualized token log-likelihoods into matching scores. Alignment is defined through context-sensitive token probabilities rather than proximity in a shared embedding space, enabling learning from sparse paired data across models with distinct vocabularies, without a shared tokenizer or decoder. LOGICA is particularly effective for mutation-local variant ranking, where comparisons reduce to context-conditioned likelihoods of mutant tokens at perturbed sites. Across protein--ligand binding, TCR--peptide activity, and drug-conditioned resistance prediction, LOGICA improves over prior state-of-the-art methods, including matched latent-contrastive and conditional MLM baselines, while retaining a token-level interface for interpretation and generation. On held-out-gene single-mutation drug-resistance prediction, LOGICA improves AUC from near-random latent-space baselines of $\sim$0.55 to $\sim$0.65.

</details>


### [119] [Trainable Photonic Measurement for Physics-Informed PDE Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18713)
*Jiale Linghu,Hao Dong,Yangshuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种光子量子神经场方法，将坐标编码为可训练的光学相位，通过多光子Fock空间干涉混合，并从光子数测量中解码。该光子电路本身作为神经场表示进行优化，而非固定特征映射或硬件加速器。在七个偏微分方程基准测试中，光子场在相位失配被残差导数放大的困难 regime 下表现最优，误差低一个数量级，且仅需约1/4的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子量子机器学习在科学机器学习中的作用尚未被充分探索。物理信息神经场为微分方程求解提供了自然场景，因为微分方程需要保留相位、频率和导数结构的试验空间。本文旨在探索光子量子测量作为科学机器学习中表示学习原理的潜力。

Method: 将坐标转化为可训练的光学相位，通过多光子Fock空间干涉混合，从光子数测量解码输出。光子电路作为神经场表示本身被优化（而非固定特征映射），并在该表示上最小化物理信息残差。

Result: 在七个椭圆、波动、非线性色散和逆PDE基准测试中，观察到相复杂度转变：经典坐标和傅里叶特征网络在平滑 regime 下足够，而光子场在残差导数放大相位失配的困难 regime 下最准确，误差低一个数量级，且仅需约1/4的可训练参数。冻结、打乱控制和噪声压力测试将性能提升归因于学习到的干涉和稳定的Fock概率读出。

Conclusion: 光子量子测量可作为一种表示学习原理用于科学机器学习，在复杂PDE问题中展现出优于经典方法的精度和参数效率。

Abstract: Photonic quantum machine learning offers a route to trainable physical representations built from phase, interference and measurement. However, its role in scientific machine learning remains largely unexplored. Physics-informed neural fields provide a natural setting, because differential equations require trial spaces that preserve phase, frequency and derivative structure. Here we introduce a photonic quantum neural field in which coordinates become trainable optical phases, are mixed by multi-photon Fock-space interference and are decoded from photon-number measurements. The photonic circuit is optimized as the neural-field representation itself, not as a fixed feature map or hardware accelerator. Photonic measurement is therefore a trainable representation on which the physics-informed residual is minimized. Across seven elliptic, wave, nonlinear dispersive and inverse PDE benchmarks, we observe a phase-complexity transition: classical coordinate and Fourier-feature networks suffice in smooth regimes, whereas the photonic field is most accurate when residual derivatives amplify phase mismatch. In the hardest regimes it gives the lowest errors, with margins reaching an order of magnitude and about one quarter of the trainable parameters of classical baselines. Frozen and shuffled controls, together with noise stress tests, attribute this gain to learned interference and stable Fock-probability readout under compound perturbations. These results identify photonic quantum measurement as a representation-learning principle for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [120] [Bayesian Anytime Pareto Set Identification for Multi-Objective Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18785)
*Lennert Saerens,Bram Silue,Eleni Litsa,Peter Vrancx,Pieter Libin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于帕累托集识别问题的任意时间多目标多臂赌博机算法——Top-Two帕累托前沿汤普森采样（TTPFTS），在合成环境、超大型分子库探索中验证其性能，并提出不确定性量化指标，同时证明算法渐近正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别帕累托最优解是支持多目标决策的关键，但现有方法多为固定预算设计，缺乏可随时停止的任意时间算法，且在多目标分子发现等实际场景中应用有限。

Method: 提出TTPFTS算法，采用贝叶斯方法；在合成环境与超大型合成型分子库中进行实验；引入不确定性量化指标以估计算法对帕累托集的置信度；提供算法渐近正确性的理论证明。

Result: TTPFTS在合成环境中优于现有固定预算算法；在超大型分子库中有效探索；不确定性量化指标能有效代理真实性能，用于监控学习进展。

Conclusion: TTPFTS是首个支持任意时间停止的多目标多臂赌博机帕累托集识别算法，兼具实证有效性与理论保证，不确定性量化方法为复杂场景下的学习监控提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Identifying Pareto optimal solutions is critical to support multi-objective decision-making. We introduce the first anytime Multi-Objective Multi-Armed Bandit algorithm for the Pareto Set Identification problem, taking a Bayesian approach: Top-Two Pareto Front Thompson Sampling (TTPFTS). We benchmark TTPFTS against state-of-the-art fixed-budget Pareto Set Identification algorithms on synthetic environments. Next, we demonstrate its practical utility in a challenging multi-objective molecular discovery setting by efficiently exploring an ultra-large synthesis-on-demand molecular library. Furthermore, we introduce a novel uncertainty quantification metric that estimates our algorithm's confidence in the predicted Pareto set. We demonstrate that this metric effectively proxies true performance, yielding a robust methodology for monitoring learning progress in complex settings. Finally, we complement these empirical findings with a theoretical proof of the algorithm's asymptotic correctness.

</details>


### [121] [Learning from Own Solutions: Self-Conditioned Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18810)
*Yingyu Shan,Yuhang Guo,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Xiangrong Zhu,Xinyi Wang,Jiashu Yao,Wei Lin,Hongru Wang,Heyan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SC-GRPO通过将自条件化轨迹产生的每token KL散度作为GRPO梯度的乘法权重，在不依赖外部教师或过程奖励模型的情况下实现细粒度信用分配，在数学、代码和智能体任务上比GRPO平均提升8.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（如GRPO）对所有token分配均匀信用，浪费梯度在常规token上而低估关键推理步骤；现有token级信用分配方法依赖过程奖励模型、真实答案或外部教师知识蒸馏，限制了在纯RLVR环境下的适用性。

Method: 提出SC-GRPO：利用模型在自条件化（基于自身已验证轨迹条件化）前后的分布差异产生的可测量每token KL散度，作为GRPO策略梯度的乘法权重，实现自监督的token级信用分配。同时理论证明了从多验证轨迹构建的自教师蒸馏会导致不可行的加权平均解。

Result: 在五个基准（数学、代码、智能体任务）上，SC-GRPO相对GRPO提升8.1%，相对DAPO提升5.9%，并超越有外部教师监督的OPD方法，且在分布外场景下表现更强。

Conclusion: SC-GRPO通过纯自监督的token级信用分配机制，在不引入外部信号的前提下显著提升了RLVR训练的有效性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has driven substantial progress in training LLMs for reasoning tasks, but representative methods such as GRPO assign uniform credit across all tokens, wasting gradient on routine tokens while under-crediting pivotal reasoning steps. Existing token-level credit assignment methods require resources beyond the model's own rollouts. GRPO variants rely on process reward models or ground-truth answers. Knowledge distillation assigns credit through per-token divergence but requires external teachers (On-Policy Distillation) or privileged information (On-Policy Self Distillation). However, these dependencies limit applicability in the pure RLVR setting. We observe that conditioning the model on its own verified trajectories induces a measurable per-token KL divergence between the original and conditioned distributions, and prove that distilling from a self-teacher constructed by verified trajectories leads to infeasible weighted-average solutions when multiple verified trajectories exist. We propose SC-GRPO (Self-Conditioned GRPO), which uses KL divergence mentioned before as a multiplicative weight on GRPO gradients. Across five benchmarks spanning math, code, and agentic tasks, SC-GRPO consistently outperforms 8.1% over GRPO and 5.9% over DAPO with stronger OOD performance. Moreover, SC-GRPO achieves higher performance than OPD.

</details>


### [122] [Low-Cost Neuromorphic Fall Detection Using Synthetic Event Data and Hybrid SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18732)
*Guillermo Rojas,Gonzalo Soto,Daniel Yunge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合脉冲神经网络（SNN）与卷积神经网络（CNN）的混合模型，将传统智能手机视频转换为模拟事件相机（DVS）数据进行人体跌倒检测，在保持精度的同时显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用SNN的能效优势和时空处理能力，结合DVS事件数据特性，解决传统视频中人体跌倒检测任务的效率瓶颈问题。

Method: 将常规视频帧模拟转换为DVS事件数据，构建SNN-CNN混合模型，通过仿真实验在多个数据集上与传统机器学习模型进行对比评估。

Result: 混合模型在保持与基线模型相当准确率的前提下，实现了显著的计算效率提升。

Conclusion: SNN与DVS技术的结合在复杂现实环境任务中具有巨大潜力，能在不牺牲精度的情况下大幅提升能效。

Abstract: This work presents the development of hybrid models that integrate spiking neural networks (SNNs) with components of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) to learn from simulated event-based camera data (Dynamic Vision Sensor, DVS) generated from conventional smartphone videos. Aimed primarily at human fall detection, the approach leverages the energy efficiency and spatio-temporal processing capabilities of SNNs by converting video frames into event-based data. The proposed models are evaluated through simulations on multiple datasets, comparing their performance to that of traditional machine learning models. Results demonstrate significant gains in efficiency without sacrificing accuracy, underscoring the potential of combining SNNs and DVS technology for complex tasks in real-world environments.

</details>


### [123] [Reinforcement Learning Foundation Models Should Already Be A Thing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18812)
*Abdelrahman Zighem,Jill-Jênn Vie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出强化学习是结构化数据基础模型中的明显缺口，提出利用MDP的充足统计量（表格形式、固定大小）与注意力架构结合，构建RL基础模型。通过纯合成MDP训练的模型在上下文学习下远优于UCB-VI和表格Q-learning，离线性能与VI-LCB相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言和视觉的基础模型依靠互联网规模数据驱动，但结构化领域（表格预测、时间序列、图学习、强化学习）不具备这样的数据条件。合成数据将负担从数据收集转向先验设计，而RL是这一范式中明显的空白——尽管采样合成MDP与采样合成表格数据集同样可行，但尚无上下文RL工作将先验设计作为首要目标。

Method: 提出MDP存在固定大小的充足统计量（独立于观测到的episode、呈表格形状），使其可直接适用于表格基础模型所用的注意力架构，只需将监督头替换为策略头。整个模型完全在合成MDP上训练。

Result: 无需任务特定微调，模型即可在上下文中解决保留的表格基准：在线设置下，所需episode数远少于UCB-VI和表格Q-learning；离线设置下，性能与VI-LCB相当。

Conclusion: 定义了RL基础模型的研究议程，证明纯合成MDP训练可实现在线/离线上下文中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Foundation models for language and vision are powered by internet-scale data, while structured domains (tabular prediction, time-series forecasting, graph learning, reinforcement learning) are not. The substitute is synthetic data, which shifts the burden from collection to prior design. Such priors already exist for many structured tasks: TabPFN and its successors solve tabular classification with a transformer pretrained on a synthetic Bayesian prior.
  We make two points. \textbf{First}, reinforcement learning is the conspicuous gap: sampling a synthetic MDP is as feasible as sampling a synthetic tabular dataset, yet no in-context RL work treats prior design as a primary objective. \textbf{Second}, MDPs admit a fixed-size sufficient statistic, independent of the episodes observed and tabular in shape, which makes them directly amenable to the attention-based architectures used for tabular foundation models, with a policy head replacing the supervised target. Together these define the agenda for an RL foundation model.
  As a proof of concept, we train one model entirely on synthetic MDPs and show that, with no task-specific tuning, it solves held-out tabular benchmarks in context, both online and offline: online, in far fewer episodes than UCB-VI and tabular Q-learning, and offline, competitively with VI-LCB.

</details>


### [124] [GateMem: Benchmarking Memory Governance in Multi-Principal Shared-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18829)
*Zhe Ren,Yibo Yang,Yimeng Chen,Zijun Zhao,Benshuo Fu,Zhihao Shu,Bingjie Zhang,Yangyang Xu,Dandan Guo,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GateMem是一个多主体共享记忆基准，评估LLM代理在多用户场景下的效用、访问控制和主动遗忘能力。实验表明，现有方法无法同时实现这三项目标，长上下文提示虽治理得分高但成本大，检索和外部记忆方法则存在信息泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准多假设单用户场景，忽略了医院、工作场所、校园和家庭等多主体共享记忆的实际部署需求。在这些场景中，多个用户写入公共记忆池并基于不同角色、范围和关系进行查询，记忆质量不仅需要检索能力，还需要治理机制。

Method: 构建GateMem基准，涵盖医疗、办公、教育和家庭四个领域，包含长篇幅多方对话、增量记忆注入、隐藏检查点、结构化评判和泄露目标标注。同时评估多种基线方法（长上下文提示、检索式方法、外部记忆方法）及不同骨干模型。

Result: 没有任何方法能同时实现强效用、鲁棒访问控制和可靠遗忘。长上下文提示在最高token成本下取得最佳治理得分，检索式和外部记忆方法虽降低成本但仍会泄露未授权或已删除信息。

Conclusion: 当前记忆代理远未达到可可靠部署于共享机构场景的水平，多主体共享记忆中的效用、访问控制和遗忘三者之间存在显著权衡。

Abstract: Memory benchmarks for LLM agents largely assume single-user settings, leaving shared assistants for hospitals, workplaces, campuses, and households understudied. In these deployments, multiple principals write to a common memory pool and query it under different roles, scopes, and relationships, so memory quality requires governance as well as recall. We introduce GateMem, a benchmark for multi-principal shared-memory agents. GateMem jointly evaluates utility for legitimate long-horizon requests with state updates, access control across contextual authorization boundaries, and agent-facing active forgetting after explicit deletion requests. It spans medical, office, education, and household domains, with long-form multi-party episodes, incremental memory injection, hidden checkpoints, structured judging, and leak-target annotations. Across diverse baselines and backbone models, no method simultaneously achieves strong utility, robust access control, and reliable forgetting. Long-context prompting often yields the best governance score at high token cost, while retrieval-based and external-memory methods reduce cost yet still leak unauthorized or deleted information. These results show current memory agents remain far from reliable shared institutional deployment.

</details>


### [125] [Maturing Markov Decision Processes: Decision Making under Increasing Information and Shrinking Action Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18820)
*Jiaxi Liu,Aiping Yang,Yuhang Yang,Shuqi Zhang,Zewei Dong,Jiangming Yang,Xuebin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出成熟马尔可夫决策过程（MMDPs），通过显式建模序贯决策中信息-动作的不对称性（随时间信息增多但动作过期），提出过期动作优先级原则，并开发了结构感知的强化学习框架。实验表明该框架能提升学习效率，尤其在规模扩大的决策问题中优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MDP将信息-动作不对称性扁平化为阶段依赖的状态描述和动作掩码，掩盖了决定哪些决策紧急、哪些可推迟的关键结构。作者希望构建一个显式建模这种不对称性的框架。

Method: 提出MMDPs形式化建模信息-动作不对称性；推导过期动作优先级原则；开发结构感知的强化学习框架，包括阶段感知策略设计、过期动作抽象、搜索增强学习与蒸馏。

Result: 在多供应商补货、简化现金管理环境及生产级仿真器上的实验表明，显式建模这种不对称性提升了学习效率，且随着决策问题规模扩大，优势更加显著。

Conclusion: MMDPs和相应的结构感知强化学习方法能有效处理信息-动作不对称性问题，在规模化决策场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Sequential decision problems often exhibit an asymmetric evolution of information and decision flexibility: as a decision cycle unfolds, the agent receives richer information while feasible actions expire due to operational cutoffs, commitments, or resource constraints. Standard MDP formulations typically flatten this structure into stage-dependent state descriptions and action masks, thereby obscuring the nested information--action asymmetry that determines which decisions are urgent and which can be deferred. We introduce Maturing Markov Decision Processes (MMDPs), a formulation built around this information--action asymmetry. We characterize one of its key consequences through an expiring-action priority principle, which identifies the actions that must be resolved before the next stage. Motivated by this structure, we develop a structure-aware reinforcement learning framework with stage-aware policy design, expiring-action abstraction, and search-augmented learning with distillation. Experiments on a controlled multi-supplier replenishment problem, simplified cash-management environments of increasing complexity, and a production-scale simulator show that explicitly modeling this asymmetry improves learning efficiency and becomes increasingly valuable as decision problems scale.

</details>


### [126] [REVES: REvision and VErification--Augmented Training for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18910)
*Yuanxin Liu,Ruida Zhou,Xinyan Zhao,Amr Sharaf,Hongzhou Lin,Arijit Biswas,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Zhaoran Wang,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REVES两阶段迭代框架，通过解耦中间步骤的"near-miss"答案生成修正和验证提示，解决标准后训练方法（单步优化）与多步推理不匹配的问题，在LiveCodeBench、数学推理和约束满足任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准后训练方法主要优化单次目标，与多步推理的动态过程存在根本性不匹配。现有方法直接优化多步轨迹，未能利用中间步骤中的高质量错误供模型学习纠正。

Method: 提出两阶段迭代框架：1) 在线数据/提示增强阶段，将成功恢复轨迹中的中间步骤（"near-miss"答案）转化为解耦的修正和验证提示；2) 策略优化阶段，集中训练有效的答案转换和错误识别。通过离策略数据生成减少长程采样的计算开销。

Result: LiveCodeBench上比RL基线高+6.5分，比标准多轮训练高+4.0分；以最小模型（4B）在circle packing上匹配此前SOTA结果且rollout更少；数学任务验证了改进的纠错能力；泛化到分布外约束满足问题（n_queens、mini_sudoku）。

Conclusion: 通过解耦中间步骤的错误修正和验证，REVES框架有效对齐了多步推理动态，实现了高效的离策略数据生成和策略优化，在多个推理任务上取得显著提升且具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling via sequential revision has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning. However, standard post-training methods primarily optimize single-shot objectives, creating a fundamental misalignment with multi-step inference dynamics. While recent work treats this as multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL), conventional approaches optimize over the multi-step trajectories directly, failing to further exploit the high-quality mistakes in intermediate steps that model can learn from correcting them. We propose a two-stage iterative framework that alternates between online data/prompt augmentation and policy optimization. By converting the intermediate steps (``near-miss'' answers) in the successful recovery trajectories into decoupled revision and verification prompts, our approach concentrates training on both effective answer transformation and error identification. This approach enables efficient off-policy data generation and reduces the computational overhead of long-horizon sampling compared to standard multi-turn RL. On LiveCodeBench, using publicly available test cases as feedback, we observe gains of +6.5 points over the RL baseline and +4.0 points over standard multi-turn training. Beyond coding, our approach matches the previously reported SOTA result on circle packing while using the smallest base model (4B) and far fewer rollouts than the much larger evolutionary search systems. Math results under ground-truth verification further confirm improved correction ability. It also generalizes to out-of-distribution constraint-satisfaction puzzles such as n\_queens and mini\_sudoku, where correctness is defined entirely by problem constraints. Code is available at https://github.com/yxliu02/REVES.git.

</details>


### [127] [RouteJudge: An Open Platform for Reproducible and Preference-Aware LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18774)
*Guannan Lai,Haoran Hu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RouteJudge是一个用于LLM路由系统的在线成对偏好评估框架，配合ORBIT工具箱，实现了路由器级别的决策质量评估和标准化开发流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注模型级别的响应质量，缺乏对路由器级别决策质量的系统性评估方法，且缺乏标准化的工作流支持路由方法的持续扩展和公平比较。

Method: RouteJudge采用匿名成对比较方式，将多个路由策略在相同模型池和预算约束下选择的响应呈现给用户，并将用户偏好归因到背后的路由策略。ORBIT提供统一的接口用于基准加载、查询表示、路由实现、预算感知评估和方法比较。

Result: 构建了包含查询、路由决策、模型响应、偏好标签、成本、延迟和任务元数据的评估记录，支持偏好感知、成本感知和任务条件分析。

Conclusion: RouteJudge和ORBIT为LLM路由系统的评估和开发提供了标准化框架，支持路由方法的提交、验证和在线偏好评估。

Abstract: We present RouteJudge, an online pairwise preference evaluation framework for LLM routing systems, with a public platform available at https://routejudge.cn. Different from model-level response evaluation, RouteJudge focuses on router-level decision quality. For each user query, multiple routing strategies independently recommend candidate models under the same model pool and budget constraints. The selected model responses are then presented to users through anonymous pairwise comparisons, and the resulting user preferences are attributed back to the routing strategies behind the compared responses. Each evaluation record stores the query, routing decisions, model responses, preference labels, cost, latency, and task metadata, enabling preference-aware, cost-aware, and task-conditioned analysis of LLM routers. To support the continuous expansion of routing methods in RouteJudge, we further release ORBIT (Optimal Routing and Budgeted Inference Toolbox), a modular and extensible toolbox that standardizes the end-to-end workflow of LLM routing. ORBIT provides unified interfaces for benchmark loading, query representation, router implementation, budget-aware evaluation, and method comparison, allowing researchers to develop and evaluate routing algorithms under consistent protocols. It also serves as the submission and integration layer for RouteJudge: researchers can implement routing methods within ORBIT, validate them on existing routing benchmarks, and submit compatible routers for online preference-based evaluation. The code of ORBIT is available at https://github.com/AIGNLAI/LAMDA-ORBIT.

</details>


### [128] [Online Distributional Prediction via Latent Cluster Geometry Under Drift and Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18778)
*Navyansh Mahla,Prateek Chanda,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜变量聚类几何的在线分布预测方法，利用吉布斯拟后验和可逆跳跃MCMC进行后验采样，通过重启机制应对概念漂移和对抗性污染，在Wasserstein-1遗憾下实现了次线性累积遗憾的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳流式数据中，传统方法通常只追踪点估计，但许多应用需要预测完整的数据生成分布。现有方法难以同时应对分布漂移和对抗性污染，且缺乏非参数化的结构保持方式。

Method: 提出潜变量聚类几何表示：每个候选分布由可变大小的中心配置（簇几何）组织概率质量并诱导预测分布。构建吉布斯拟后验对这些配置进行后验平均，使用可逆跳跃MCMC采样可变维后验。针对漂移提出重启变体，在时间上局部化拟贝叶斯更新。

Result: 在支持集有界、潜几何稳定、预测映射正则、oracle可实现、重启窗口局部化、传输作用次线性、污染预算次线性的条件下，重启预测器实现次线性累积Wasserstein遗憾。遗憾界分解为PAC-Bayesian复杂度项、污染敏感的后验扰动项和动态最优传输项。

Conclusion: 该方法无需对数据流、漂移机制或污染过程指定参数化模型，即可在非平稳流式分布预测中获得可证明的次线性遗憾保证，同时保留结构化潜空间以处理不确定性、正则化和比较。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary streams is often formulated as tracking a point estimate, but many applications require predicting the full data-generating distribution. We study online distributional prediction under drift and adversarial corruption. Our approach represents each candidate law through a latent cluster geometry: a variable-size configuration of centers that organizes probability mass and induces a predictive distribution. A Gibbs quasi-posterior over these configurations yields an online predictor by posterior averaging, and the resulting variable-dimensional posterior can be sampled with reversible-jump MCMC. The method therefore avoids specifying a parametric streaming law while retaining a structured latent space for uncertainty, regularization, and comparison.
  We evaluate performance by cumulative Wasserstein-1 regret against the time-varying true law. The analysis separates two effects: corruption perturbs the loss-based posterior update, whereas drift makes long-horizon posterior memory stale. We address the latter with a restarted variant that temporally localizes the same quasi-Bayesian update. The resulting high-probability bounds decompose into a PAC-Bayesian complexity term, a corruption-sensitive posterior perturbation term, and a dynamic optimal-transport term driven by \(A_T^{\mathrm{OT}}=\sum_{t=2}^T W_2^2(p_{t-1}^*,p_t^*)\). Under bounded support, stable latent geometry, predictive-map regularity, oracle realizability, localized restart windows, sublinear transport action, and sublinear corruption budget, the restarted predictor achieves sublinear cumulative Wasserstein regret. These guarantees require no parametric model for the stream, drift mechanism, or corruption process.

</details>


### [129] [Target-confidence Recourse Using tSeTlin machines: TRUST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18832)
*K. Darshana Abeyrathna,Sara El Mekkaoui,Nils Enric Canut Taugbøl,Anuja Vats*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST框架，利用概率Tsetlin机器和贝叶斯优化，允许用户指定目标置信度来生成反事实解释，而非仅跨越决策边界。实验表明该方法能产生更鲁棒、可解释的补救措施，在多个基准上实现完美鲁棒性同时保持低补救成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法通常只寻求最小化输入改动以翻转模型决策，但仅跨越决策边界的反事实在噪声或模型变化下脆弱且不稳定。实际决策系统不仅依赖预测标签，还依赖置信度阈值和风险边际，因此需要能显式控制预测置信度的反事实生成方法。

Method: 提出TRUST框架，结合概率Tsetlin机器(PTM)与贝叶斯优化，直接搜索满足用户定义置信度目标的最小输入改动。利用PTM的基于子句的概率结构将预测置信度与决策规则的稳定性关联起来，通过子句激活的稳健性揭示决策是鲁棒还是脆弱的支持。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，目标置信度反事实比传统基于边界的方产生更鲁棒和可解释的补救措施。在多个基准上实现完美鲁棒性且保持低补救成本，例如在Haberman数据集上以0.92置信度达到0.10的L2距离。

Conclusion: 通过显式控制置信度并暴露规则级别的稳定性，TRUST为高风险决策支持提供了可操作的补救措施，弥补了传统基于边界方法在鲁棒性和可靠性方面的不足。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations are widely used to provide algorithmic recourse in high-stakes decision-making systems. Most existing methods seek the smallest change to an input that flips a model's decision. However, decision-makers often rely not only on predicted labels but also on confidence thresholds and risk margins. Counterfactuals that barely cross a decision boundary can be fragile and unstable under noise or model variation.
  In this paper, we propose Target-confidence Recourse Using tSeTlin machines (TRUST), a framework in which users explicitly specify the desired prediction confidence for recourse. Rather than generating counterfactuals and evaluating confidence afterward, TRUST directly searches for minimal changes that satisfy a user-defined confidence target, enabling comparison of recourse options in terms of cost, confidence, and robustness.
  We instantiate TRUST using a Probabilistic Tsetlin Machine (PTM) combined with Bayesian optimization. The probabilistic clause-based structure of PTM links prediction confidence to the stability of decision rules. We show that counterfactuals satisfying the same rules can still differ substantially in reliability depending on how securely they satisfy those rules, revealing whether decisions are supported by robust or fragile clause activations.
  Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that target-confidence counterfactuals produce more robust and interpretable recourse than conventional boundary-based approaches. Across multiple benchmarks, TRUST achieves perfect robustness while maintaining low recourse cost, including an L2 distance of 0.10 on the Haberman dataset at 0.92 confidence. By explicitly controlling confidence and exposing rule-level stability, TRUST provides actionable recourse for high-stakes decision support.

</details>


### [130] [STARE: Surprisal-Guided Token-Level Advantage Reweighting for Policy Entropy Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19236)
*Haipeng Luo,Qingfeng Sun,Songli Wu,Can Xu,Wenfeng Deng,Han Hu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO训练中策略熵坍塌问题，本文通过梯度分析发现token级别的信用分配不匹配，并提出STARE方法，利用surprisal分位数识别关键token子集、重加权优势函数并引入目标熵闭环门控，在1.5B-32B模型上稳定训练数千步，在AIME24/25上超越DAPO等基线4%-8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等RVR算法在复杂推理任务中虽表现优异，但普遍存在训练过程中策略熵坍塌的问题，限制了探索-利用平衡和RL训练潜力。

Method: STARE方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过batch内surprisal分位数识别熵关键token子集；(2) 选择性重加权这些token的有效优势函数；(3) 引入目标熵闭环门控机制实现稳定的熵调控。

Result: 在1.5B到32B参数量级、三个任务族（Short CoT、Long CoT、多轮工具使用）上，STARE能在数千步内维持稳定RL训练且熵保持在目标区间内；在AIME24和AIME25上，相比DAPO等基线平均准确率提升4%-8%。

Conclusion: STARE通过细粒度的token级熵调控有效解决了策略熵坍塌问题，维持了探索-利用平衡，进一步释放了RL训练潜力，reflection token和响应长度同步增长验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards algorithms like GRPO have emerged as the dominant post-training paradigm for complex reasoning in LLMs, yet commonly suffer from policy entropy collapse during training. We conduct a first-order gradient analysis of token-level entropy dynamics under GRPO and identify a token-level credit assignment mismatch: the per-token entropy variation decomposes into the product of the trajectory-level advantage and an entropy sensitivity function over the next-token distribution, yielding an advantage-surprisal four-quadrant structure and a near-criticality property. Motivated by it, we propose STARE (Surprisal-guided Token-level Advantage Reweighting for policy Entropy stability), which identifies entropy-critical token subsets via batch-internal surprisal quantiles, selectively reweights their effective advantages, and incorporates a target-entropy closed-loop gate for stable entropy regulation. Across model scales from 1.5B to 32B and three task families (Short CoT, Long CoT, and Multi-Turn Tool Use), STARE sustains stable RL training over thousands of steps while maintaining policy entropy within the target band. On AIME24 and AIME25, STARE outperforms DAPO and other competitive baselines by 4%-8% in average accuracy, with reflection tokens and response length growing in tandem, indicating sustained exploration-exploitation balance that further unlocks RL training potential.Code is available at https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE.

</details>


### [131] [Scaling Learning-based AEB with Massive Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18864)
*Xiangyu Wang,Yang Zhan,Mengxiang Hao,Chuanchuan Zhong,Yansong Jia,Junjie Zhang,Yu Han,Xin Jiang,Zhen Cao,Ying Wang,Yulun Song,Zhitao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于元反馈半监督学习（MF-SSL）的规模化自动紧急制动（AEB）方法，通过噪声感知解耦和运动学门控伪标签策略解决生产环境中的锚点模糊和标注-未标注数据不匹配问题，在1B窗口数据上训练并部署至数十万辆车辆，实超100:1的正误触发比和35%的无事故驾驶里程提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在生产约束下利用海量未标注车队数据规模化训练基于学习的自动紧急制动（AEB）系统，同时解决标注数据稀缺和系统泛化难题。

Method: 提出稳定化元反馈半监督学习（Stabilized MF-SSL）框架，包含：(i) 噪声感知解耦（Noise-Aware Decoupling），从教师模型的监督更新路径中移除易产生歧义的锚点；(ii) 运动学门控伪标签（Kinematics-Gated Pseudo-Labeling）结合教师冲突惩罚，抑制未标注数据上的风险幻觉，同时保持广泛覆盖。

Result: 未标注数据规模从1M扩展到1B窗口时性能持续提升；1B训练的模型部署至数十万辆车辆，超10^9 km验证，正误触发比超100:1，无事故驾驶里程比纯规则基线提升35%。

Conclusion: 提出的稳定化MF-SSL框架能有效利用大规模未标注车队数据提升AEB系统的安全性能，在保持舒适性的同时显著降低误触发，证明了半监督学习在自动驾驶安全关键感知系统中的规模化应用可行性。

Abstract: This paper studies how to scale learning-based automatic emergency braking (AEB) with massive unlabeled fleet data under production constraints. Our approach is based on meta-feedback semi-supervised learning (MF-SSL), where a teacher generates pseudo labels for unlabeled driving data and is updated using a small labeled anchor set as safety-critical feedback. In production, anchor ambiguity and labeled-unlabeled mismatch can amplify systematic pseudo-label errors, leading to spurious triggers. We propose a stabilized MF-SSL framework with (i) Noise-Aware Decoupling, which removes ambiguity-prone anchors from the teacher's supervised update path, and (ii) kinematics-gated pseudo-labeling with a teacher conflict penalty to suppress mismatch-induced risk hallucinations on unlabeled data while maintaining broad coverage. Extensive experiments show consistent gains as unlabeled data scale from 1M to 1B windows, improving safety while keeping comfort stable. The 1B-trained student model is deployed to hundreds of thousands of vehicles and validated over \$10^9$ km of driving, achieving a positive-to-false activation ratio exceeding 100:1 and a 35% improvement in accident-free driving mileage over a production rule-only baseline.

</details>


### [132] [Structured Inference with Large Language Gibbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19264)
*Sanghyeok Choi,Henry Gouk,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Large Language Gibbs (LLG)方法，利用LLM的条件分布作为MCMC中的转移算子进行结构化概率推理，通过迭代重采样单个变量来避免单次自回归生成中的顺序偏差，产生平衡所有局部条件分布的平稳分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码的世界知识可用于结构化推理，但以概率一致的方式访问这些知识面临困难，现有单次自回归生成存在顺序依赖偏差，无法保证概率一致性。

Method: 将LLM的next-token条件分布用作吉布斯采样的转移算子，在结构化变量上迭代重采样每个变量（条件于其他变量），形成马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样过程。

Result: 在合成分布采样、一致性推理任务和贝叶斯结构学习上验证，表明LLM条件分布结合MCMC是单次生成的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: 在噪声LLM条件分布下，基于MCMC的迭代采样方法能够实现比单次自回归生成更稳健的结构化概率推理。

Abstract: The knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs) can serve as a substrate for structured reasoning over variables describing a complex world, but accessing this knowledge in a probabilistically coherent manner poses a difficult inference problem. We propose Large Language Gibbs, a scheme for structured probabilistic inference that uses conditional distributions of an LLM as transition operators. Rather than sampling structured objects through single-pass autoregressive generation, we iteratively resample individual variables conditioned on others using an LLM's next-token conditionals. This approach avoids order-dependent biases and produces a stationary distribution that reflects a compromise between all local conditionals. We apply this approach to sampling from synthetic distributions, consistent reasoning tasks, and Bayesian structure learning. The results suggest that the use of LLM conditionals in MCMC is a practical alternative to one-pass generation for structured probabilistic inference under a world prior accessible through noisy LLM conditionals.

</details>


### [133] [Domain-Shift Aware Neural Networks for Unbalance Characterization in Rotating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18882)
*Bernardo Feijó Junqueira,Claudio Kiyoshi Umezu,Bruno Bilhar Karaziack,Tomaz Junior,Daniel Alves Castello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向域偏移感知的神经网络在旋转轴不平衡质量估计回归任务中的应用，通过最大均值差异策略对齐源域与目标域特征分布，实验证明该方法能有效提升未知工况下的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转轴在变工况下运行，域偏移（如不同转速、辅助轴启停）导致训练条件与测试条件不匹配，传统模型难以准确估计不平衡质量。本文旨在解决域偏移对回归任务精度的影响，特别是当系统物理行为及域偏移来源不完全已知时。

Method: 采用域偏移感知神经网络，将不平衡质量估计的逆问题构建为域自适应框架；利用最大均值差异（MMD）策略对齐源域与目标域的特征表示；实验数据来自配备三轴加速度计的测试台，主轴携带不平衡质量法兰在不同转速下运行，并通过可选辅助轴引入域差异。

Result: 显式处理域偏移显著提升了预测精度，尤其在系统物理行为及域偏移来源超出训练条件且不完全已知的情况下效果明显。

Conclusion: 域偏移感知模型在结构健康监测的回归任务中具有重要潜力，为变工况下旋转机械的不平衡质量估计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This work investigates the application of a domain-shift aware neural network for regression tasks aimed at estimating unbalance masses in rotating shafts under varying operating conditions. Experimental data were collected from a test rig in which a primary shaft, equipped with a flange carrying unbalanced masses, was driven at different rotational speeds, while a secondary shaft could be optionally activated to introduce domain discrepancy. The unbalance masses were positioned at a fixed radial distance, and the dynamic response of the system was recorded using triaxial accelerometers. The inverse problem of mass estimation is formulated within a domain adaptation framework, where the network is trained with a maximum mean discrepancy strategy to align feature representations across source and target distributions. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of explicitly addressing domain shift in improving prediction accuracy, especially when the system's physical behavior and sources of domain discrepancy are not fully known and fall outside the training conditions. These findings highlight the potential of domain-shift aware models for regression tasks in Structural Health Monitoring.

</details>


### [134] [A Controlled Benchmark of Quantum-Latent GAN Augmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18970)
*Syed Mujtaba Haider,Silvia Figini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格控制参数数量的对比基准（量子生成器1648参数 vs 经典生成器1632参数），系统评估了量子生成模型在脑部MRI图像增强中的效果。在5%-100%标签数据比例、8个随机种子的严格统计检验下，发现量子与经典生成器的增强效果无显著差异，且均未显著优于仅使用真实数据的基线。合成样本在数据稀缺时存在分布外偏移和模式坍塌，量子生成器在多样性上亦无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于量子生成模型用于医学图像增强的研究常声称能提升分类精度，但这些结论往往基于单次训练、未匹配量子与经典生成器的参数预算、且未明确增强方法有效的具体数据区间。因此需要建立一个受控的、严格的基准测试来独立评估量子生成器的实际贡献。

Method: 将脑部MRI图像编码到KL正则化的潜在空间，在该空间中训练条件Wasserstein GAN（带梯度惩罚），分别使用变分量子生成器（1648参数）和参数近似的经典生成器（1632参数）。生成的合成样本经解码后用于增强预训练分类器，在5%-100%共8种标签数据比例下，基于8个随机种子进行配对显著性检验（含多重比较校正），同时分析集内多样性和潜在空间分布。

Result: 在所有标签数据比例下，没有任何一种增强变体显著优于仅使用真实数据训练；量子与经典生成器在统计上不可区分。任何低数据下的表现提升本质上是正则化效应而非忠实的数据扩展——合成样本在数据稀缺时呈现分布外偏移和严重模式坍塌，且量子生成器的多样性与经典生成器相比并无优势。

Conclusion: 量子生成模型在医学图像增强中并未展现出优于经典方法或超越无增强基线的实际效果，当前宣称的精度提升可能源于不严谨的实验设计。作者将所提出的评估协议作为量子生成增强在医学影像中严格测试的标准基准公开发布。

Abstract: Medical image classification is often constrained by limited labeled data, motivating generative augmentation; recently, quantum generative models have been proposed for this purpose, frequently reporting accuracy gains. However, such claims are typically based on single training runs, do not match the parameter budgets of the quantum and classical generators, and do not characterize the data regime in which any benefit appears. We present a controlled benchmark that isolates the contribution of a quantum generator to brain-MRI augmentation. Images are encoded into a KL-regularized latent space in which a conditional Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty is trained using either a variational quantum generator or a classical generator of near-identical parameter count (1648 vs. 1632). Synthetic samples are decoded and used to augment a pretrained classifier across labeled data fractions from 5% to 100%, evaluated over eight random seeds with paired significance testing (with multiple-comparison correction) and with intraset diversity and latent-distribution analyses. Across all fractions, no augmentation variant significantly outperforms real-data-only training, and the quantum and classical generators are statistically indistinguishable. Any low-data benefit behaves as regularization rather than faithful data expansion:synthetic samples are off distribution and severely mode collapsed precisely where data is scarce, and the quantum generator is no more diverse thanits classical counterpart. We release the protocol as a testbed for rigorous evaluation of quantum generative augmentation in medical imaging.

</details>


### [135] [Seed-Guided Semi-Supervised Clustering by A-Contrario Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18833)
*Nassir Mohammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于异常检测与聚类统计对偶性的半监督聚类框架，通过Perception算法（期望阈值𝔼<1）实现无参数调优的聚类，在仅需10-30个种子标签的情况下，对含噪声、离群点和新兴未知簇的数据展现出优异性能，并保持线性时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法在噪声环境中面临挑战：划分型算法容易过度指派离群点，密度型算法对全局启发式参数敏感。需要一种鲁棒的聚类定义和算法，能够在无参数调优的情况下有效处理噪声、离群点和未知簇。

Method: 基于a-contrario统计推理和Gestalt邻近原理，将聚类定义为相对于均匀随机性零假设的无异常最大子集。核心是Perception算法，利用期望阈值（𝔼<1）识别离群点。采用"聚类-排除"迭代机制：通过少量用户种子标签初始化鲁棒簇中心，然后迭代纳入非异常点。利用t-SNE、UMAP等邻域保持嵌入进行特征表示。

Result: 在合成和真实基准（图像、文本数据集，使用原始、线性降维和邻域保持嵌入表示）上评估。仅需每簇10-30个种子，在低调优基准协议下达到有竞争力且常为极强的性能，同时在固定种子簇数和迭代次数下保持关于样本量和维度的线性可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的半监督聚类框架有效解决了噪声环境下的鲁棒聚类问题，通过统计对偶性将异常检测与聚类统一，在极小标注成本下实现高性能，且无需手动参数调优，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a semi-supervised clustering framework grounded in the statistical duality between grouping principles and anomaly detection. We address the challenge of robust cluster definition in noisy environments -- a task where partitioning algorithms often over-assign outliers and density-based methods remain sensitive to heuristic global parameters. Drawing on \textit{a-contrario} statistical reasoning and Gestalt proximity principles, we define a cluster as a maximal subset of data points containing no anomalies relative to a null hypothesis of uniform randomness. Central to this approach is the Perception algorithm, which utilises a principled expectation-based threshold ($\mathbb{E} < 1$) to identify outliers without manual parameter tuning. By treating clustering as the dual of anomaly detection, we employ an iterative ``clustering-by-exclusion'' mechanism. The algorithm is seed-guided, leveraging minimal user-provided labels to initialise robust cluster medians and form initial groups, which are subsequently expanded by admitting non-anomalous points. This approach naturally isolates fringe points, isolated noise, and emerging unknown clusters. We evaluate the method on synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including image and text datasets represented through raw, linear-reduced, and neighbourhood-preserving embeddings. Results demonstrate that with as few as 10--30 seeds per cluster, the proposed method achieves competitive and often very strong performance under a practical low-tuning benchmarking protocol, while maintaining linear scalability with respect to both observations and dimensionality for a fixed number of seeded clusters and iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Identifying Structural Biases from Causal Mechanism Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18834)
*Praharsh Nanavati,Jilles Vreeken,David Kaltenpoth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过因果机制变化来识别隐藏混淆和选择偏差的方法，并设计了StruBI算法，在合成和真实数据上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法通常假设数据独立同分布且无未观测变量，但实际中这些假设常被违反，导致推断不准确。本文旨在从因果机制变化中识别隐藏混淆和选择偏差。

Method: 提出基于互信息的可检验准则，通过分析不同环境下变量机制的变化来区分无偏变量、受隐藏混淆影响的变量和受选择偏差影响的变量，并据此设计了StruBI算法。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，StruBI能够准确恢复受影响变量集和偏差类型，性能大幅优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 结构偏差会导致依赖的机制变化，通过互信息准则可以识别各类结构偏差，StruBI算法在实践中表现良好。

Abstract: Causal discovery methods commonly assume that all data is independently and identically distributed (i.i.d.) and that there are no unmeasured variables affecting the system. In practice, these assumptions are often violated, leading to inaccurate inference. In this paper, we study how to identify hidden confounding and selection biases from causal mechanism shifts. In particular, we show that structural biases lead to dependent mechanism shifts. That is, by considering for which variables the mechanisms change given data from different environments, we can tell which variables are unbiased, which are subject to hidden confounding, and which are undergoing selection bias. We formalize this into an empirically testable criterion based on mutual information, and show under which conditions it identifies structural biases. To tell which nodes are subject to what kind of bias, we introduce the StruBI algorithm. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that StruBI works well in practice, accurately recovering affected variable sets and types of biases, outperforming the state-of-the-art by a wide margin.

</details>


### [137] [FoMoE: Breaking the Full-Replica Barrier with a Federation of MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19025)
*Lorenzo Sani,Zeyu Cao,Meghdad Kurmanji,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanovic,Yan Gao,Wanru Zhao,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoMoE提出了一种打破全副本范式的方法，通过将专家层划分到不同工作节点上，实现了跨地理分布式数据中心训练大规模MoE模型，相比基线降低了高达45倍的通信成本并提升了1.4倍的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模LLM预训练依赖紧密耦合的硬件加速器，而分布式数据并行方法（如DiLoCo、Photon）要求每个站点维护完整模型副本，导致巨大的内存约束和通信开销，无法高效利用地理上分散、弱连接的数据中心资源。

Method: FoMoE系统采用专家层跨工作节点分区的策略，打破了全模型副本的范式。具体包括：(I) 通过部分专家复制减少通信成本；(II) 提出skip-token机制提升经验吞吐量；(III) 在训练代理规模下验证路由稳定性，并通过系统建模推断到100B参数规模。

Result: 在研究的设定下：(I) 通信成本相比高效基线降低1.42倍，相比DDP降低45.44倍；(II) 通过skip-token机制实现高达1.4倍的吞吐量加速；(III) 在训练代理规模下路由稳定，系统建模显示通信和内存优势可扩展至100B参数配置。

Conclusion: FoMoE通过分区专家层有效解决了分布式MoE训练中全副本范式的低效问题，在通信效率和吞吐量上取得了显著提升，并展现出良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Pre-training Large Language Models (LLMs) typically demands large-scale infrastructure with tightly coupled hardware accelerators. While increasing model and dataset scale remains the dominant driver of performance, Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) architectures have recently achieved state-of-the-art results by decoupling parameter count from computational cost. This efficiency enables training massive models on constrained compute budgets, yet it typically requires the high-speed interconnects of a single datacenter. To overcome these physical limits, recent approaches such as DiLoCo and Photon use low-communication data-parallel methods to enable scaling across geographically distributed, weakly connected data centers. However, these methods suffer from a fundamental inefficiency: they require full model replicas at every site, which imposes prohibitive memory constraints and communication overheads. In this work, we introduce FoMoE, a system that breaks the full-replica paradigm by partitioning expert layers across workers. We demonstrate that FoMoE: (I) reduces communication costs by up to 1.42x over efficient baselines and 45.44x over DDP via partial expert replication in the studied regimes; (II) achieves empirical throughput speedups of up to 1.4x through a novel skip-token mechanism; and (III) shows stable routing in the trained proxy regimes and projects the communication/memory benefits to 100B-scale configurations through system modelling.

</details>


### [138] [Semantic Robustness Certification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18839)
*Peiyu Yang,Paul Montague,Feng Liu,Andrew C. Cullen,Amardeep Kaur,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个针对视觉语言模型在语义层面变换下的鲁棒性认证框架，利用文本提示作为语义代理，通过封闭形式决策边界量化认证模型预测不变性，无需额外数据即可适用于多种语义变化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性认证框架主要关注几何或像素级变换，而实际应用中视觉语言模型常面临语义层面的分布偏移（如形状、大小、风格等），缺乏针对语义级变换的认证方法。

Method: 利用视觉语言模型的开放词汇能力，将文本提示作为语义代理构造由程度参数控制的语义变换；通过刻画模型决策边界的封闭形式，量化认证预测类别不变的语义变换程度区间。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该框架能够在多种语义变化场景下有效认证鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个无需为每种变化额外数据即可认证视觉语言模型语义级鲁棒性的框架，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are now widely used in downstream tasks. However, real-world applications often expose VLMs to distribution shifts induced by semantic variation (e.g., shape, size, and style). Robustness certification determines if a model's prediction changes when transformations are applied to its input. While most certification frameworks study geometric or pixel-level transformations over inputs, this work proposes a novel framework that enables certifying VLM robustness under semantic-level transformations. Leveraging the open-vocabulary capability of VLMs, we use text prompts as semantic proxies to construct transformations parameterized by an extent that controls the degree of semantic variation. By characterizing the VLM decision boundary in closed form, our framework quantitatively certifies extent intervals for which the predicted class remains unchanged under the semantic transformation. Our framework is the first to certify VLM robustness under semantic-level variations without requiring additional data for each variation, making it practical to apply. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data show that our framework enables certifying robustness under diverse semantic variations across scenarios.

</details>


### [139] [A Hybrid LSTM--Vision Transformer Architecture for Predicting HRRR Forecast Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19026)
*David Aaron Evans,Jay C. Rothenberger,Kara J. Sulia,Nick P. Bassill,Chris D. Thorncroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合LSTM-Vision Transformer（LSTM-ViT）框架，通过融合地表观测时序数据与纽约州中网探测仪获取的大气廓线数据，预测高分辨率快速刷新（HRRR）模型在降水、10米风速和2米气温方面的预报误差。相比基线LSTM模型，该框架在所有三个预报变量上均提升了误差预测能力，尤其在对流增强的边界层活动期间表现更为显著，降水预报误差的预测技能提升了约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率数值天气预报（NWP）系统中的预报误差常与未解析的边界层过程、对流、地形诱导环流等垂直结构化大气现象相关。前期研究表明LSTM可利用地表观测预测HRRR预报误差，但其性能在复杂垂直大气演化期间会退化，亟需引入垂直结构信息来改进误差预测。

Method: 构建混合LSTM-Vision Transformer（LSTM-ViT）框架：LSTM分支处理地表观测的时序序列以捕获时间依赖性，ViT分支处理大气廓线数据以捕获垂直结构特征，两者融合后预测HRRR模型在降水、10米风速和2米气温的预报误差。

Result: 相比基线LSTM，LSTM-ViT框架在所有三个预报变量上均提升了预报误差预测技能；短期预报时效和增强的PBL活动期间改进最大；降水预报误差的预测技能提升约两倍，能更好捕获对流驱动的误差演化并减少与PBL过程相关的退化。

Conclusion: 将时序学习与垂直注意力机制相结合为改善运行NWP系统中的预报误差预测提供了一条物理上有意义的路径，可为预报员提供关于模式偏差和预报置信度的增强指导。

Abstract: Forecast errors in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) systems are often linked to unresolved planetary boundary layer (PBL) processes, convection, terrain-induced circulations, and other vertically structured atmospheric phenomena. Previous work demonstrated that Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can successfully predict forecast errors in the High-Resolution Rapid Refresh (HRRR) model using mesonet observations, but we believe performance degradation is linked to periods of complex vertical atmospheric evolution. To address this limitation, we develop a hybrid LSTM-Vision Transformer (LSTM-ViT) framework that combines temporal sequence learning from surface observations with atmospheric profiles from the New York State Mesonet profiler network. The LSTM-ViT framework is trained to predict HRRR hourly precipitation, 10 m wind speed, and 2 m temperature forecast errors at individual mesonet stations. Across all three predictors, incorporation of profiler-derived atmospheric structure improves forecast error prediction skill relative to the baseline LSTM architecture, with the largest gains occurring at shorter forecast lead times and during periods of enhanced PBL activity. Improvements are particularly pronounced for precipitation forecast error, where the LSTM-ViT framework achieves approximately a twofold increase in predictive skill relative to the baseline LSTM while better capturing convectively driven error evolution and reducing degradation associated with PBL processes. These results demonstrate that combining temporal sequence learning with vertically informed attention mechanisms provides a physically meaningful pathway for improving forecast error prediction in operational NWP systems. Our research offers forecasters enhanced guidance regarding model bias and forecast confidence.

</details>


### [140] [Learning from Your Own Mistakes: Constructing Learnable Micro-Reflective Trajectories for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18844)
*Zhilin Huang,Hang Gao,Ziqiang Dong,Yuan Chen,Yifeng Luo,Chujun Qin,Jingyi Wang,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAPO通过显式构建对比轨迹和微反射修正，将自我蒸馏从隐式分布对齐推进到显式轨迹构建，在AIME 2024/2025和HMMT 2025上一致优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我蒸馏方法通过隐式logit级别的KL散度对齐进行分布模仿，无法诊断模型的具体错误或提供针对个体失败模式的纠正指导，缺乏细粒度的修正信号。

Method: TAPO在RL训练中同时生成正确和错误的rollouts，利用对比结构构建微反射修正轨迹：保留错误推理直到失败点，插入自然语言诊断和基于正确参考的纠正推理。引入难度感知候选选择和去耦优势估计以防止梯度污染。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上，TAPO在相同训练步数下一致优于GRPO，并增强了首次推理能力和错误修正效果。

Conclusion: TAPO通过显式轨迹修正替代隐式分布对齐，更有效地利用模型自身的推理信号进行自我改进，为自我蒸馏提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Self-distillation improves reasoning in large language models by using the model's own rollouts as training signal, typically through implicit logit-level alignment that minimizes KL divergence toward a privileged target distribution. However, because this supervision is generated via uncontrolled sampling, it provides no diagnostic insight into the model's specific errors or corrective guidance for its individual failure patterns. Consequently, the model learns to imitate a privileged distribution rather than receiving fine-grained corrections that pinpoint where and why its reasoning fails. In this paper, we propose Trajectory-Augmented Policy Optimization (TAPO), which advances self-distillation from implicit distributional alignment to explicit trajectory construction. During RL training, the model produces both correct and incorrect rollouts to the same query, and TAPO leverages this contrastive structure to construct micro-reflective corrections, new training trajectories that retain the model's erroneous reasoning up to the point of failure, then insert a natural-language diagnosis and corrected reasoning guided by a correct reference from the same sampling group. Since each trajectory is anchored in the learner's own prefix and solutions, the corrective signal preserves the model's on-policy distribution to a greater extent than the position-wise alignment imposed by KL-based methods. To integrate these trajectories, TAPO introduces difficulty-aware candidate selection at the model's capability boundary and decoupled advantage estimation to prevent gradient contamination. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025 show that TAPO achieves consistent improvements over GRPO under the same number of training steps. Further analysis demonstrates that TAPO strengthens both first-pass reasoning and error-correction effectiveness.

</details>


### [141] [Investigating Inductive Biases for Machine Learning Emulation of Sudden Stratospheric Warmings in Idealised Isca Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18857)
*Oskar Bohn Lassen,Simon Driscoll,Stephen I. Thomson,Sebastian Schemm,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究测试了不同机器学习架构（卷积、Transformer、图神经网络）对平流层突然增温（SSW）动力学的模拟能力，发现三维垂直耦合的归纳偏置对平流层动力学仿真至关重要，但低预测误差并不能保证物理上准确的波-流相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索机器学习仿真器能否利用平流层变率等可预测性锚点来提升次季节至季节尺度的预报技巧，并评估不同架构的归纳偏置对平流层突然增温动力学仿真的影响。

Method: 使用成对的理想化Isca模拟（仅改变施加的波-2加热扰动），训练卷积、Transformer和图神经网络三种架构进行单步预测，比较它们在平流层活跃期与平静期的表现差异，并通过Eliassen-Palm通量诊断评估物理保真度。

Result: 在平流层动力学平静时各架构差异不大，但在SSW-like变率活跃时差异显著增大；明确的三维垂直耦合是仿真平流层动力学的关键归纳偏置；低预测误差不能保证物理上准确的波-平均流相互作用，平流层波驱动结构仍存在相干误差。

Conclusion: 三维垂直耦合的归纳偏置对机器学习平流层动力学仿真至关重要，但仅关注预测误差不足以保证物理一致性，需结合物理诊断指标来评估模型表现。

Abstract: Machine-learning emulators are increasingly used for weather prediction and have the potential to extend skill on subseasonal-to-seasonal timescales by learning dynamically important sources of predictability. A key challenge is whether the models can exploit predictability anchors, such as stratospheric variability, that influence tropospheric circulation beyond short lead times. We test how architectural inductive bias affects emulation of sudden stratospheric warming (SSW) dynamics using paired idealised Isca simulations that differ only in an imposed wave-2 heating perturbation. Across convolutional, transformer, and graph-based architectures trained for one-step prediction, model differences are modest when the stratosphere is dynamically quiet but widen substantially when SSW-like variability is active. Our results identify explicit three-dimensional vertical coupling as a key inductive bias for machine-learning emulation of stratospheric dynamics. However, Eliassen-Palm flux diagnostics show that low forecast error does not guarantee physically faithful wave-mean-flow interaction, with coherent errors remaining in stratospheric wave-driving structure.

</details>


### [142] [Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19134)
*Arnaud Lequen,Clément Legrand-Lixon,Léo Saulières*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PQLRM结合了帕累托Q学习与奖励机，提出了一种用于非马尔可夫奖励结构的多目标强化学习算法，实验证明其收敛速度快于基线方法，并能合成QRM无法获得的帕累托最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如PQL基线或QRM）在非马尔可夫、由奖励机编码的多目标奖励结构下，难以同时兼顾样本效率与帕累托最优策略的合成能力。

Method: 将帕累托Q学习（保持向量值Q估计集以近似帕累托前沿）与QRM中利用奖励机自动机结构因式分解奖励信号的思想相结合，得到多策略算法PQLRM。

Result: 在实验中，PQLRM比直接应用于跨乘积MDP的朴素PQL基线收敛更快，并且能够合成QRM无法找到的帕累托最优策略。

Conclusion: 将奖励机的结构化信息融入多目标强化学习可以有效提升样本效率和策略质量，为处理非马尔可夫多目标奖励任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines (PQLRM), a multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm for tasks whose reward structure is specified by a set of reward machines (RMs). PQLRM combines Pareto Q-Learning (PQL), which maintains sets of vector-valued Q-estimates to approximate the Pareto front, with enhancements from Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM), which exploits the factored automaton structure of the reward signal. This yields a multi-policy algorithm that remains sample-efficient under non-Markovian, RM-encoded rewards. Experimental trials show that PQLRM converges faster than a naive PQL baseline applied to the cross-product MDP and can synthesize Pareto-optimal policies that QRM cannot.

</details>
